@echo off
cd /d "%~dp0"

echo Running tree.py...
python tree.py

if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo tree.py failed. Stopping.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo tree.py finished successfully.
echo Running script.py...

python script.py

if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo script.py failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo Both scripts completed successfully.
pause
---END FILE---

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\tree.py
CONTENT:
import json
from pathlib import Path

EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    "node_modules",
    ".git",
    "dist",
    "build",
    ".next",
    ".cache",
    "ref",
    "bin",
    "obj"
}

MAX_DEPTH = None  # set to int (e.g. 5) or None for unlimited
OUTPUT_FILE = "folder_structure.txt"


def build_tree(path: Path, depth: int = 0, max_depth=None):
    if max_depth is not None and depth > max_depth:
        return None

    if path.is_dir():
        if path.name in EXCLUDE_DIRS:
            return {
                "type": "directory",
                "name": path.name,
                "excluded": True
            }

        node = {
            "type": "directory",
            "name": path.name,
            "children": []
        }

        try:
            entries = sorted(
                path.iterdir(),
                key=lambda p: (p.is_file(), p.name.lower())
            )
        except PermissionError:
            return None

        for entry in entries:
            child = build_tree(entry, depth + 1, max_depth)
            if child is not None:
                node["children"].append(child)

        return node

    return {
        "type": "file",
        "name": path.name
    }


if __name__ == "__main__":
    root_path = Path(".").resolve()

    tree = {
        "type": "directory",
        "name": root_path.name,
        "root": True,
        "children": []
    }

    for item in sorted(
        root_path.iterdir(),
        key=lambda p: (p.is_file(), p.name.lower())
    ):
        node = build_tree(item, depth=1, max_depth=MAX_DEPTH)
        if node is not None:
            tree["children"].append(node)

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(tree, f, indent=2)

    print(f"Folder structure written to {OUTPUT_FILE}")

---END FILE---

import "./fonts.css";
import "./index.css";

const queryClient = new QueryClient();

ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <Provider store={store}>
      <PersistGate persistor={persistor} loading={null}>
        <ModalProvider>
          <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
            <App />

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\api\axiosInterceptors.js
CONTENT:
import axiosInstance from "./axiosInstance.js";
import store from "../app/store.js";
import { logout, setCredentials } from "../features/client/account/auth/authslice.js";

const publicPaths = new Set([
  "/auth/send-otp",
  "/auth/verify-otp",
  "/auth/login",
  "/auth/refresh",
  "/auth/forgot-password",
  "/auth/reset-password",
  "/auth/google",
]);

const isPublicEndpoint = (url = "") => {
  try {
    const target = new URL(url, axiosInstance.defaults.baseURL ?? "https://localhost:5001/api");
    return [...publicPaths].some((publicPath) => target.pathname.endsWith(publicPath));
  } catch {
    return [...publicPaths].some((publicPath) => url.endsWith(publicPath));
  }
};

axiosInstance.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const requestUrl = config.url ?? "";
  const token = store.getState().auth?.accessToken;

  if (token && !isPublicEndpoint(requestUrl)) {
    config.headers = {
      ...config.headers,
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    };
  }

  return config;
});

let isRefreshing = false;
let refreshWaiters = [];

axiosInstance.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  async (error) => {
    const originalRequest = error.config;

    if (!originalRequest || !error.response || isPublicEndpoint(originalRequest.url ?? "")) {
      return Promise.reject(error);
    }

    if (error.response.status === 401 && !originalRequest._retry) {
      if (isRefreshing) {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          refreshWaiters.push({ resolve, reject });
        })
          .then(() => axiosInstance(originalRequest))
          .catch((retryError) => Promise.reject(retryError));
      }

      originalRequest._retry = true;
      isRefreshing = true;

      try {
        const refreshResponse = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/refresh", {}, { withCredentials: true });
        const nextToken = refreshResponse.data?.accessToken ?? refreshResponse.data?.token;

        if (!nextToken) {
          throw new Error("Refresh response did not include a new access token.");
        }

        store.dispatch(setCredentials({ accessToken: nextToken }));
        originalRequest.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${nextToken}`;

        refreshWaiters.forEach(({ resolve }) => resolve());
        refreshWaiters = [];

        return axiosInstance(originalRequest);
      } catch (refreshError) {
        refreshWaiters.forEach(({ reject }) => reject(refreshError));
        refreshWaiters = [];
        store.dispatch(logout());
        return Promise.reject(refreshError);
      } finally {
        isRefreshing = false;
      }
    }

    return Promise.reject(error);
  },
);

CONTENT:
export const unwrapList = (value) => {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
  if (Array.isArray(value?.items)) return value.items;
  if (Array.isArray(value?.data)) return value.data;
  return value;
};

export const invalidate = (queryClient, keys) =>
  keys.forEach((queryKey) => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }));

import axiosInstance from "../axiosInstance.js";

export const sendOtp = async ({ email }) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/send-otp", { email });
  return response.data;
};

export const verifyOtp = async ({ email, otp, password, fullName, role }) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/verify-otp", {
    email,
    otp,
    password,
    fullName,
    role,
  });
  return response.data;
};

export const loginUser = async ({ email, password }) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/login", { email, password });
  return response.data;
};

export const getCurrentUser = async () => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.get("/auth/me");
  return response.data;
};

export const logoutUser = async () => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/logout");
  return response.data;
};

export const refreshToken = async () => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/refresh");
  return response.data;
};

export const forgotPassword = async ({ email }) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/forgot-password", { email });
  return response.data;
};

export const resetPassword = async ({ email, token, newPassword }) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/reset-password", {
    email,
    token,
    newPassword,
  });
  return response.data;
};

export const googleAuth = async ({ idToken, role }) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/google", { idToken, role });
  return response.data;
};

export const requestDeleteOtp = async () => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/request-delete-otp");
  return response.data;
};

export const deleteAccount = async ({ otp }) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/delete-account", { otp });
  return response.data;
};

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\app\store.js
CONTENT:
import { configureStore } from "@reduxjs/toolkit";
import { combineReducers } from "redux";
import { persistReducer, persistStore } from "redux-persist";
import storage from "redux-persist/lib/storage";
import authReducer from "../features/client/account/auth/authslice.js";

const rootReducer = combineReducers({
  auth: persistReducer(
    {
      key: "auth",
      storage,
      whitelist: ["user", "accessToken"],
    },
    authReducer,
  ),
});

const resettableRootReducer = (state, action) => {
  if (action.type === "auth/logout" || action.type === "auth/logout/fulfilled") {
    storage.removeItem("persist:auth");
    state = undefined;
  }
  return rootReducer(state, action);
};

const store = configureStore({
  reducer: resettableRootReducer,
  middleware: (getDefaultMiddleware) =>
    getDefaultMiddleware({
      serializableCheck: false,
    }),
});

export const persistor = persistStore(store);
export default store;

---END FILE---

import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

function isActivePath(pathname, href) {
  if (href === "/") return pathname === "/";
  return pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);
}

export default function InfrastructureShell({ eyebrow, title, navigation = [], children, footer = "WebOfWork" }) {
  const location = useLocation();

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <header className="app-shell__header">
        <Link className="app-shell__brand" to="/">
          <span className="app-shell__brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">W</span>
          <span>WebOfWork</span>
        </Link>
        <div className="app-shell__header-copy">
          <span className="app-shell__eyebrow">{eyebrow}</span>
          <strong>{title}</strong>
        </div>
      </header>
      <div className="app-shell__body">
        <aside className="app-shell__sidebar" aria-label={`${title} navigation`}>
          <nav className="app-shell__nav">
            {navigation.map((item) => (
              <Link className={isActivePath(location.pathname, item.href) ? "app-shell__link app-shell__link--active" : "app-shell__link"} key={item.href} to={item.href}>
                <span>{item.label}</span>
                {item.meta && <small>{item.meta}</small>}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>
        </aside>
        <main className="app-shell__main">{children}</main>
      </div>
      <footer className="app-shell__footer">{footer}</footer>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useChatSocket } from "../../hooks/chat/UseChatRooms.jsx";
import { useSendChatMessage, useMarkChatRoomRead } from "./chatMutations.js";
import { useChatMessages, useChatRooms } from "./chatQueries.js";

const layoutStyle = {
  display: "grid",
  gridTemplateColumns: "280px minmax(0, 1fr)",
  minHeight: "640px",
  border: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
  borderRadius: "20px",
  overflow: "hidden",
  background: "#fff",
  boxShadow: "0 10px 30px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06)",
};

const sidebarStyle = {
  borderRight: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
  background: "#f8fafc",
  padding: "1rem",
};

const roomButtonStyle = (active) => ({
  width: "100%",
  border: active ? "1px solid #0f172a" : "1px solid transparent",
  background: active ? "#eef2ff" : "transparent",
  borderRadius: "12px",
  padding: "0.8rem 0.9rem",
  textAlign: "left",
  cursor: "pointer",
  marginBottom: "0.65rem",
  color: "#0f172a",
});

const contentStyle = {
  display: "flex",
  flexDirection: "column",
  minHeight: 0,
};

const headerStyle = {
  padding: "1rem 1.25rem",
  borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
  display: "flex",
  alignItems: "center",
  justifyContent: "space-between",
  gap: "1rem",
};

const messagesStyle = {
  flex: 1,
  overflowY: "auto",
  padding: "1.25rem",
  display: "flex",
  flexDirection: "column",
  gap: "0.85rem",
  background: "#f8fafc",
};

const messageBubbleStyle = (mine) => ({
  maxWidth: "72%",
  alignSelf: mine ? "flex-end" : "flex-start",
  background: mine ? "#0f172a" : "#ffffff",
  color: mine ? "#ffffff" : "#0f172a",
  border: mine ? "1px solid #0f172a" : "1px solid #e2e8f0",
  borderRadius: "14px",
  padding: "0.7rem 0.9rem",
  lineHeight: 1.5,
  boxShadow: "0 4px 10px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.04)",
});

const composerStyle = {
  borderTop: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
  padding: "1rem 1.25rem",
  display: "flex",
  gap: "0.75rem",
  background: "#fff",
};

const inputStyle = {
  flex: 1,
  border: "1px solid #cbd5e1",
  borderRadius: "10px",
  padding: "0.8rem 0.9rem",
  fontSize: "0.95rem",
  outline: "none",
};

const buttonStyle = {
  border: "none",
  background: "#0f172a",
  color: "#fff",
  borderRadius: "10px",
  padding: "0.8rem 1rem",
  fontWeight: 600,
  cursor: "pointer",
};

export default function Chat() {
  const { data: rooms = [] } = useChatRooms();
  const [selectedRoomId, setSelectedRoomId] = useState(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");

  const selectedRoom = useMemo(
    () => rooms.find((room) => room.id === selectedRoomId) ?? rooms[0] ?? null,
    [rooms, selectedRoomId],
  );

  const { data: messages = [] } = useChatMessages(selectedRoom?.id);
  const sendMessage = useSendChatMessage();
  const markRoomRead = useMarkChatRoomRead();
  const { joinRoom } = useChatSocket();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedRoom?.id) {
      joinRoom(selectedRoom.id);
    }
  }, [joinRoom, selectedRoom?.id]);

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!selectedRoom || !draft.trim()) {
      return;
    }

    sendMessage.mutate(
      { roomId: selectedRoom.id, content: draft.trim() },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setDraft("");
          markRoomRead.mutate({ roomId: selectedRoom.id, messageId: null });
        },
      },
    );
  };

  if (!rooms.length) {
    return (
      <section style={{ padding: "2rem", background: "#fff", border: "1px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: "20px" }}>
        <span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Messages</span>
        <h1 style={{ margin: "0.5rem 0 0.5rem" }}>No active conversations yet</h1>
        <p style={{ margin: 0, color: "#475569" }}>Open a contract or conversation to start messaging.</p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section style={layoutStyle} aria-label="Chat conversations">
      <aside style={sidebarStyle}>
        <span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Conversations</span>
        <h2 style={{ margin: "0.75rem 0 1rem", fontSize: "1.5rem" }}>Inbox</h2>

        {rooms.map((room) => (
          <button
            key={room.id}
            type="button"
            style={roomButtonStyle(selectedRoom?.id === room.id)}
            onClick={() => setSelectedRoomId(room.id)}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: "0.2rem" }}>
              {room.title ?? room.participantName ?? `Room ${room.id}`}
            </div>
            <div style={{ color: "#475569", fontSize: "0.82rem" }}>
              {room.lastMessage ?? "No messages yet"}
            </div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </aside>

      <div style={contentStyle}>
        <header style={headerStyle}>
          <div>
            <div className="app-shell__eyebrow">Chat</div>
            <h3 style={{ margin: "0.25rem 0 0" }}>{selectedRoom?.title ?? selectedRoom?.participantName ?? "Conversation"}</h3>
          </div>
          <span style={{ color: "#475569", fontSize: "0.8rem" }}>
            {messages.length} message{messages.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
          </span>
        </header>

        <div style={messagesStyle}>
          {messages.length === 0 ? (
            <div style={{ color: "#475569", margin: "auto", textAlign: "center" }}>
              Start the conversation with a quick introduction.
            </div>
          ) : (
            messages.map((message) => {
              const mine = message.isMine ?? message.senderId === selectedRoom?.currentUserId;
              const messageKey = message.id ?? `${message.createdAt ?? "unknown"}-${message.senderName ?? "user"}-${message.content ?? "content"}`;
              return (
                <div key={messageKey} style={messageBubbleStyle(mine)}>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.72rem", opacity: 0.8, marginBottom: "0.25rem" }}>
                    {message.senderName ?? (mine ? "You" : "Participant")}
                  </div>
                  <div>{message.content ?? ""}</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.68rem", opacity: 0.78, marginTop: "0.35rem", textAlign: mine ? "right" : "left" }}>
                    {message.createdAt ? new Date(message.createdAt).toLocaleString() : "Just now"}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={composerStyle}>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={draft}
            onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Write a message..."
            style={inputStyle}
            aria-label="Message text"
          />
          <button type="submit" disabled={sendMessage.isPending || !draft.trim()} style={{ ...buttonStyle, opacity: sendMessage.isPending || !draft.trim() ? 0.6 : 1 }}>
            {sendMessage.isPending ? "Sending..." : "Send"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import axiosInstance from "../../api/axiosInstance.js";
export const fetchChatRooms = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/chat/rooms")).data;
export const createChatRoom = async (contractId) => (await axiosInstance.post("/chat/rooms", { contractId })).data;
export const fetchChatRoom = async (roomId) => (await axiosInstance.get(`/chat/rooms/${roomId}`)).data;
export const fetchChatMessages = async ({ roomId, pageSize = 50, beforeMessageId }) => (await axiosInstance.get(`/chat/rooms/${roomId}/messages`, { params: { pageSize, beforeMessageId } })).data;
export const sendChatMessage = async ({ roomId, content }) => (await axiosInstance.post(`/chat/rooms/${roomId}/messages`, { content })).data;
export const deleteChatMessage = async ({ roomId, messageId }) => (await axiosInstance.delete(`/chat/rooms/${roomId}/messages/${messageId}`)).data;
export const archiveChatRoom = async (roomId) => (await axiosInstance.patch(`/chat/rooms/${roomId}/archive`)).data;
export const markChatRoomRead = async ({ roomId, messageId }) => (await axiosInstance.patch(`/chat/rooms/${roomId}/read`, { messageId })).data;

export function formatChatMessage(message, currentUserId) {
  if (!message || !String(message.content ?? "").trim()) return null;
  return { ...message, isMine: message.isMine ?? message.senderId === currentUserId, displayTime: message.createdAt ? new Date(message.createdAt).toLocaleString() : "Just now" };
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\components\chat\chatMutations.js
CONTENT:
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { archiveChatRoom, createChatRoom, deleteChatMessage, markChatRoomRead, sendChatMessage } from "./chatApi.js";
const refresh = (client, roomId) => { client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["chat-rooms"] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["chat-room", roomId] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["chat-messages", roomId] }); };
export const useCreateChatRoom = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: createChatRoom, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["chat-rooms"] }) }); };
export const useSendChatMessage = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: sendChatMessage, onSuccess: (_, variables) => refresh(client, variables.roomId) }); };
export const useDeleteChatMessage = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteChatMessage, onSuccess: (_, variables) => refresh(client, variables.roomId) }); };
export const useArchiveChatRoom = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: archiveChatRoom, onSuccess: (data, roomId) => refresh(client, roomId) }); };
export const useMarkChatRoomRead = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: markChatRoomRead, onSuccess: (_, variables) => refresh(client, variables.roomId) }); };

---END FILE---

export const useChatMessages = (roomId, options = {}) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["chat-messages", roomId, options], queryFn: () => fetchChatMessages({ roomId, ...options }), enabled: Boolean(roomId) });

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\components\common\GlobalErrorBoundary.jsx
CONTENT:
import { Component } from "react";

export default class GlobalErrorBoundary extends Component {
  constructor(props) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error, errorInfo) {
    console.error("Unhandled app error:", error, errorInfo);
  }

  handleReset = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null });
    window.location.reload();
  };

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div style={{
          minHeight: "100vh",
          display: "grid",
          placeItems: "center",
          padding: "2rem",
          background: "#f8fafc",
          color: "#0f172a",
          fontFamily: "Inter, sans-serif",
        }}>
          <div style={{
            maxWidth: "560px",
            width: "100%",
            border: "1px solid #e2e8f0",
            borderRadius: "20px",
            background: "#fff",
            boxShadow: "0 18px 40px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.08)",
            padding: "2rem",
          }}>
            <div style={{ marginBottom: "1rem", color: "#dc2626", fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: "0.08em", textTransform: "uppercase", fontSize: "0.72rem" }}>
              Something went wrong
            </div>
            <h1 style={{ margin: "0 0 0.75rem", fontSize: "2rem" }}>The app hit an unexpected error.</h1>
            <p style={{ margin: "0 0 1.25rem", color: "#475569", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
              Please refresh the page or try again. If the issue continues, contact support.
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={this.handleReset}
              style={{
                border: "none",
                borderRadius: "10px",
                background: "#0f172a",
                color: "#fff",
                padding: "0.8rem 1.1rem",
                cursor: "pointer",
                fontWeight: 700,
              }}
            >
              Reload app
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\components\common\SupportPage.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { createSupportTicket } from "./commonApi.js";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export default function SupportPage() {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ subject: "", message: "" });
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const set = (key, value) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }));
  const submit = async (event) => { event.preventDefault(); if (!form.subject.trim() || !form.message.trim()) return toast.error("Please fill in all required fields."); setPending(true); try { await createSupportTicket({ subject: form.subject.trim(), message: form.message.trim() }); toast.success("Support ticket created successfully."); setForm({ subject: "", message: "" }); } catch (error) { toast.error(error?.response?.data?.error || "Failed to send message."); } finally { setPending(false); } };
  return <section className="content-section"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Support</span><h1>Contact support</h1><p>Send us a message and our team will respond to your registered email address.</p><form className="form-card" onSubmit={submit}><label>Subject<input required value={form.subject} onChange={(e) => set("subject", e.target.value)} /></label><label>Message<textarea required rows={6} value={form.message} onChange={(e) => set("message", e.target.value)} /></label><button className="button button--accent" disabled={pending}>{pending ? "Sending..." : "Send message"}</button></form></section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useFAQs } from "./faqQuries.js";
import PublicPageFrame from "../PublicPageFrame.jsx";

const categories = [["", "All"], ["general", "General"], ["freelancer", "Freelancers"], ["client", "Clients"], ["payment", "Payments"]];

export default function FAQ() {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState(null);
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useFAQs({ category });
  const items = data?.items ?? data?.Items ?? [];

  return <PublicPageFrame><section className="editorial-hero editorial-hero--compact"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Answers, without the maze</span><h1>Frequently asked questions.</h1><p>Find practical answers about projects, professionals, payments, and support.</p></section><section className="content-section faq-section"><div className="filter-row">{categories.map(([value, label]) => <button key={value} type="button" className={category === value ? "filter-chip filter-chip--active" : "filter-chip"} onClick={() => { setCategory(value); setOpenId(null); }}>{label}</button>)}</div>{isLoading && <div className="content-state">Loading answers…</div>}{isError && <div className="content-state">Answers are temporarily unavailable.</div>}{!isLoading && !isError && items.length === 0 && <div className="content-state">No questions are available for this category.</div>}<div className="faq-list">{items.map((item) => <article className="faq-item" key={item.id}><button type="button" aria-expanded={openId === item.id} onClick={() => setOpenId(openId === item.id ? null : item.id)}><span>{item.question}</span><b>{openId === item.id ? "−" : "+"}</b></button>{openId === item.id && <p>{item.answer}</p>}</article>)}</div></section></PublicPageFrame>;
}

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\components\notifications\Notifications.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useNotificationSocket } from "../../hooks/notification/useNotificationSocket.jsx";
import { useDeleteNotification, useMarkAllNotificationsRead, useMarkNotificationRead } from "./notificationMutations.js";
import { useNotifications } from "./notificationQueries.js";

const cardStyle = {
  border: "1px solid #e2e8f0",
  borderRadius: "16px",
  background: "#fff",
  padding: "1rem 1.1rem",
  marginBottom: "0.9rem",
  boxShadow: "0 8px 22px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.04)",
};

const unreadStyle = {
  borderLeft: "4px solid #2563eb",
};

export default function Notifications() {
  const { data: notifications = [] } = useNotifications({ page: 1, limit: 20 });
  const markRead = useMarkNotificationRead();
  const markAllRead = useMarkAllNotificationsRead();
  const deleteNotification = useDeleteNotification();
  useNotificationSocket();

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((notification) => !notification.isRead).length;

  return (
    <section style={{ padding: "0.25rem 0" }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: "1rem", marginBottom: "1.25rem" }}>
        <div>
          <span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Alerts</span>
          <h1 style={{ margin: "0.4rem 0 0" }}>Notifications</h1>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => markAllRead.mutate()}
          disabled={markAllRead.isPending || unreadCount === 0}
          style={{ border: "1px solid #cbd5e1", borderRadius: "10px", padding: "0.7rem 1rem", background: "#fff", cursor: markAllRead.isPending || unreadCount === 0 ? "not-allowed" : "pointer" }}
        >
          Mark all read
        </button>
      </div>

      {notifications.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ ...cardStyle, color: "#475569" }}>No notifications yet.</div>
      ) : (
        notifications.map((notification) => (
          <article
            key={notification.id}
            style={{
              ...cardStyle,
              ...(notification.isRead ? {} : unreadStyle),
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: "1rem", alignItems: "start" }}>
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: "0.3rem" }}>{notification.title ?? "Update"}</div>
                <div style={{ color: "#475569", lineHeight: 1.5 }}>{notification.message ?? notification.body ?? "No details provided."}</div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "0.5rem", alignItems: "center" }}>
                {!notification.isRead && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => markRead.mutate(notification.id)}
                    disabled={markRead.isPending}
                    style={{ border: "1px solid #cbd5e1", borderRadius: "8px", background: "#fff", padding: "0.45rem 0.7rem", cursor: markRead.isPending ? "not-allowed" : "pointer" }}
                  >
                    Read
                  </button>
                )}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => deleteNotification.mutate(notification.id)}
                  disabled={deleteNotification.isPending}
                  style={{ border: "1px solid #fecaca", borderRadius: "8px", background: "#fff1f2", color: "#991b1b", padding: "0.45rem 0.7rem", cursor: deleteNotification.isPending ? "not-allowed" : "pointer" }}
                >
                  Delete
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style={{ marginTop: "0.75rem", fontSize: "0.78rem", color: "#64748b" }}>
              {notification.createdAt ? new Date(notification.createdAt).toLocaleString() : "Recently"}
            </div>
          </article>
        ))
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\admin\auth\AdminLogin.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useDispatch, useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { Navigate, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { loginUser, logoutUser } from "../../../api/auth/authApi.js";
import { logout, setCredentials } from "../../client/account/auth/authslice.js";
import { validateAdminLogin } from "./adminLoginSchema.js";

export default function AdminLogin() {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
  const [values, setValues] = useState({ email: "", password: "" });
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user && String(user.role ?? user.Role) === "3") navigate("/admin", { replace: true });
  }, [navigate, user]);

  if (user && String(user.role ?? user.Role) === "3") return <Navigate to="/admin" replace />;

  const update = (event) => setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [event.target.name]: event.target.value }));
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const nextErrors = validateAdminLogin(values);
    setErrors(nextErrors);
    setServerError("");
    if (Object.keys(nextErrors).length) return;

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const response = await loginUser(values);
      const role = response.role ?? response.Role;
      if (String(role) !== "3" && String(role).toLowerCase() !== "admin") {
        setServerError("These credentials do not belong to an administrator.");
        await logoutUser().catch(() => undefined);
        dispatch(logout());
        return;
      }
      dispatch(setCredentials({ accessToken: response.accessToken ?? response.AccessToken, user: { id: response.userId ?? response.UserId, email: response.email ?? response.Email, fullName: response.fullName ?? response.FullName, role } }));
      navigate("/admin", { replace: true });
    } catch (error) {
      setServerError(error.response?.data?.detail ?? error.response?.data?.message ?? "Unable to sign in with these credentials.");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return <main className="admin-auth-page"><section className="admin-auth-panel"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Restricted workspace</span><h1>Admin sign in</h1><p>Review platform health, accounts, and money movement from one quiet control room.</p><form onSubmit={submit} noValidate><label>Email<input name="email" type="email" value={values.email} onChange={update} autoComplete="email" />{errors.email && <small>{errors.email}</small>}</label><label>Password<input name="password" type="password" value={values.password} onChange={update} autoComplete="current-password" />{errors.password && <small>{errors.password}</small>}</label>{serverError && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{serverError}</div>}<button className="button button--accent" type="submit" disabled={submitting}>{submitting ? "Checking access..." : "Enter admin workspace"}</button></form></section></main>;
}
---END FILE---

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\admin\auth\adminLoginSchema.js
CONTENT:
export function validateAdminLogin(values) {
  const errors = {};
  if (!values.email || !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(values.email)) errors.email = "Enter a valid email address.";
  if (!values.password || values.password.length < 6) errors.password = "Password must be at least 6 characters.";
  return errors;
}

CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { LayoutGrid, Wrench } from "lucide-react";
import { AddCategoryModal } from "./components/AddCategoryModal.jsx";
import { DeleteCategoryModal } from "./components/DeleteCategoryModal.jsx";
import { EditCategoryModal } from "./components/EditCategoryModal.jsx";
import AddSkillModal from "./components/AddSkillsModal.jsx";
import DeleteSkillModal from "./components/DeleteSkillModal.jsx";
import EditSkillModal from "./components/EditSkillsModal.jsx";
import { ManagementList } from "./ManagementList.jsx";
import { useAllCategories, useCategories, useSkills } from "./categoryQueries.js";
import { useModal } from "../../../hooks/modal/useModalStore.jsx";

export default function CategoriesAndSkills() {
  const { modal, modalProps, closeModal, openModal } = useModal();
  const [categoryPage, setCategoryPage] = useState(1);
  const [skillPage, setSkillPage] = useState(1);
  const [categoryInput, setCategoryInput] = useState("");
  const [skillInput, setSkillInput] = useState("");
  const [categorySearch, setCategorySearch] = useState("");
  const [skillSearch, setSkillSearch] = useState("");

  const { data: categoryData, isLoading: catLoading } = useCategories({ page: categoryPage, search: categorySearch });
  const { data: allCategoriesData = [], isLoading: categoriesListLoading } = useAllCategories();
  const { data: skillData, isLoading: skillLoading } = useSkills({ page: skillPage, search: skillSearch });

  if ((catLoading && !categoryData) || (skillLoading && !skillData)) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center text-sm text-slate-500">
        Loading management data...
      </div>
    );
  }

  const categories = Array.isArray(categoryData) ? categoryData : categoryData?.results ?? [];
  const filteredCategories = categories.filter((category) =>
    category.name.toLowerCase().includes(categorySearch.toLowerCase()),
  );

  const skills = Array.isArray(skillData) ? skillData : skillData?.results ?? [];
  const filteredSkills = skills.filter((skill) => skill.name.toLowerCase().includes(skillSearch.toLowerCase()));

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 p-8 md:p-12">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <ManagementList
          title="Categories"
          icon={LayoutGrid}
          items={filteredCategories}
          iconColor="blue"
          searchValue={categoryInput}
          onSearchChange={setCategoryInput}
          onSearchSubmit={() => {
            setCategorySearch(categoryInput.trim());
            setCategoryPage(1);
          }}
          currentPage={categoryPage}
          totalCount={filteredCategories.length}
          onNext={() => setCategoryPage((page) => page + 1)}
          onPrev={() => setCategoryPage((page) => Math.max(page - 1, 1))}
          hasNext={false}
          hasPrev={categoryPage > 1}
          onAdd={() => openModal("add-category")}
          onEdit={(item) => openModal("edit-category", item)}
          onDelete={(item) => openModal("delete-category", item)}
        />

        <ManagementList
          title="Skills"
          icon={Wrench}
          items={filteredSkills}
          iconColor="purple"
          searchValue={skillInput}
          onSearchChange={setSkillInput}
          onSearchSubmit={() => {
            setSkillSearch(skillInput.trim());
            setSkillPage(1);
          }}
          currentPage={skillPage}
          totalCount={filteredSkills.length}
          onNext={() => setSkillPage((page) => page + 1)}
          onPrev={() => setSkillPage((page) => Math.max(page - 1, 1))}
          hasNext={false}
          hasPrev={skillPage > 1}
          onAdd={() => openModal("add-skill", { allCategories: allCategoriesData })}
          onEdit={(item) => openModal("edit-skill", { item, allCategories: allCategoriesData })}
          onDelete={(item) => openModal("delete-skill", item)}
        />
      </div>

      {modal === "add-category" && <AddCategoryModal open onOpenChange={(value) => !value && closeModal()} />}
      {modal === "edit-category" && <EditCategoryModal open onOpenChange={(value) => !value && closeModal()} category={modalProps} />}
      {modal === "delete-category" && <DeleteCategoryModal open onOpenChange={(value) => !value && closeModal()} category={modalProps} />}
      {modal === "add-skill" && (
        <AddSkillModal categories={allCategoriesData} isLoading={categoriesListLoading} onClose={closeModal} />
      )}
      {modal === "edit-skill" && (
        <EditSkillModal open onOpenChange={(value) => !value && closeModal()} skill={modalProps.item} categories={modalProps.allCategories ?? []} />
      )}
      {modal === "delete-skill" && <DeleteSkillModal open onOpenChange={(value) => !value && closeModal()} skill={modalProps} />}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createCategory, createSkill, deleteCategory, deleteSkill, updateCategory, updateSkill } from "./categoryApi.js";
const useRefresh = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return () => { client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["categories"] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-categories"] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-skills"] }); }; };
export const useCreateCategory = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: createCategory, onSuccess: refresh }); };
export const useUpdateCategory = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ id, data }) => updateCategory(id, data), onSuccess: refresh }); };
export const useDeleteCategory = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteCategory, onSuccess: refresh }); };
export const useCreateSkill = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: createSkill, onSuccess: refresh }); };
export const useUpdateSkill = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ id, data }) => updateSkill(id, data), onSuccess: refresh }); };
export const useDeleteSkill = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteSkill, onSuccess: refresh }); };

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, GripVertical, Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";

const iconTone = {
  blue: "text-blue-600",
  purple: "text-purple-600",
};

export function ManagementList({
  title,
  icon,
  items,
  iconColor,
  onAdd,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
  searchValue,
  onSearchChange,
  onSearchSubmit,
  onNext,
  onPrev,
  hasNext,
  hasPrev,
  currentPage,
  totalCount,
}) {
  const IconComponent = icon;

  return (
    <div className="mb-12">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3 shadow-sm">
            <IconComponent className={`h-6 w-6 ${iconTone[iconColor] || "text-slate-600"}`} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{items.length} {title.toLowerCase()}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onAdd}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-sm transition-colors hover:bg-blue-700"
        >
          <Plus size={18} />
          Add {title}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <input
          value={searchValue}
          onChange={(event) => onSearchChange(event.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(event) => {
            if (event.key === "Enter") {
              onSearchSubmit();
            }
          }}
          placeholder={`Search ${title.toLowerCase()}...`}
          className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-sm text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="group flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-5 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50/80"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <GripVertical className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-300" />
                <span className="text-[15px] font-medium text-slate-700">{item.name}</span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onEdit(item)}
                  className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-600"
                  aria-label={`Edit ${item.name}`}
                >
                  <Pencil size={18} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onDelete(item)}
                  className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
                  aria-label={`Delete ${item.name}`}
                >
                  <Trash2 size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {items.length === 0 && (
            <div className="px-6 py-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">No {title.toLowerCase()} found</div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {totalCount > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-2 md:flex-row">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Showing {items.length} of {totalCount} results</p>
          <nav className="flex items-center rounded-md shadow-sm">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={!hasPrev}
              onClick={onPrev}
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-l-md border border-slate-300 bg-white transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <ChevronLeft className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
            </button>
            <div className="flex h-9 items-center border-y border-blue-600 bg-blue-600 px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-white">
              {currentPage}
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={!hasNext}
              onClick={onNext}
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-r-md border border-slate-300 bg-white transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <ChevronRight className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
            </button>
          </nav>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\admin\categorymanagement\components\AddCategoryModal.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useCreateCategory } from "../categoryMutations.js";

export function AddCategoryModal({ open, onOpenChange }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const createMutation = useCreateCategory();

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const trimmedName = name.trim();

    if (!trimmedName) {
      toast.error("Category name is required.");
      return;
    }

    createMutation.mutate(
      { name: trimmedName },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setName("");
          toast.success("Category created.");
          onOpenChange(false);
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          const message = error?.response?.data?.error || error?.response?.data?.detail || "Failed to create category.";
          toast.error(message);
        },
      },
    );
  };

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/70 p-4">
      <div className="w-[440px] rounded-xl bg-white p-8 shadow-2xl">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800">Add Category</h2>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input
            value={name}
            onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Web Development"
            className="h-12 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
          />

          {createMutation.isError && (
            <p className="text-xs text-red-600">
              {createMutation.error?.response?.data?.error || createMutation.error?.response?.data?.detail || "Failed to create category."}
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 py-3 font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 py-3 font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              disabled={createMutation.isPending}
            >
              {createMutation.isPending ? "Adding..." : "Add"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useCreateSkill } from "../categoryMutations.js";

export default function AddSkillModal({ categories = [], isLoading, onClose }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState("");
  const createSkillMutation = useCreateSkill();

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const trimmedName = name.trim();

    if (!trimmedName || !categoryId) {
      toast.error("Please fill in all fields.");
      return;
    }

    createSkillMutation.mutate(
      {
        name: trimmedName,
        categoryId: Number(categoryId),
        isCustom: false,
      },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.success("Skill created successfully.");
          setName("");
          setCategoryId("");
          onClose();
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          const message = error?.response?.data?.error || error?.response?.data?.detail || "Failed to create skill.";
          toast.error(message);
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const handleCancel = () => {
    setName("");
    setCategoryId("");
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-[448px] rounded-xl bg-white shadow-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-8 pb-6 pt-8">
          <h2 className="text-[17px] font-semibold text-slate-800">Add Skill</h2>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleCancel} className="text-slate-400 transition hover:text-slate-600">
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-8 pb-8">
          <p className="mb-8 text-xs text-slate-600">Add a new skill to the platform catalog.</p>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Skill Name</label>
              <input
                value={name}
                onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. React"
                className="h-[50px] w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Category</label>
              <select
                disabled={isLoading}
                value={categoryId}
                onChange={(event) => setCategoryId(event.target.value)}
                className="h-12 w-full appearance-none rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              >
                {isLoading ? (
                  <option>Loading categories...</option>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <option value="">Select category</option>
                    {categories.map((category) => (
                      <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>
                        {category.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </>
                )}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCancel}
                className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 py-3 font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={createSkillMutation.isPending || isLoading}
                className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-600 py-3 font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {createSkillMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin" /> : "Add Skill"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useDeleteCategory } from "../categoryMutations.js";

export function DeleteCategoryModal({ onOpenChange, category }) {
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteCategory();

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!category) return;

    deleteMutation.mutate(category.id, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        toast.success("Category deleted.");
        onOpenChange(false);
      },
      onError: (error) => {
        const message = error?.response?.data?.error || error?.response?.data?.detail || "Failed to delete category.";
        toast.error(message);
      },
    });
  };

  if (!category) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/70 p-4">
      <div className="w-[440px] rounded-xl bg-white p-8 shadow-2xl">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800">Delete Category</h2>

        <p className="mb-6 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Are you sure you want to delete <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{category.name}</span>?
        </p>

        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
            className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 py-3 font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-200"
          >
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleDelete}
            className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-red-600 py-3 font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-red-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            disabled={deleteMutation.isPending}
          >
            {deleteMutation.isPending ? "Deleting..." : "Delete"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\admin\categorymanagement\components\DeleteSkillModal.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useDeleteSkill } from "../categoryMutations.js";

export default function DeleteSkillModal({ open, onOpenChange, skill }) {
  const deleteSkillMutation = useDeleteSkill();
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  if (!open || !skill) return null;

  const handleDelete = () => {
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    deleteSkillMutation.mutate(skill.id, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        toast.success("Skill deleted.");
        onOpenChange(false);
      },
      onError: (error) => {
        const message = error?.response?.data?.error || error?.response?.data?.detail || "Failed to delete skill.";
        toast.error(message);
      },
      onSettled: () => setIsSubmitting(false),
    });
  };

  const handleCancel = () => {
    onOpenChange(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-[448px] overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-white shadow-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-8 pb-6 pt-8">
          <h2 className="text-[17px] font-semibold text-slate-800">Delete Skill</h2>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleCancel} className="text-slate-400 transition hover:text-slate-600">
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-8 pb-8">
          <p className="mb-4 text-xs text-slate-600">Are you sure you want to delete this skill?</p>

          <div className="mb-6 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-4 py-4">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">{skill.name}</p>
          </div>

          <p className="mb-8 text-xs text-slate-500">This action cannot be undone.</p>

          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCancel}
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 py-3 font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleDelete}
              disabled={deleteSkillMutation.isPending || isSubmitting}
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-red-500 py-3 font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-red-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {deleteSkillMutation.isPending || isSubmitting ? "Deleting..." : "Delete Skill"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useUpdateCategory } from "../categoryMutations.js";

export function EditCategoryModal({ onOpenChange, category }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(category?.name ?? "");
  const updateMutation = useUpdateCategory();

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const trimmedName = name.trim();

    if (!category || !trimmedName) {
      toast.error("Category name is required.");
      return;
    }

    updateMutation.mutate(
      { id: category.id, data: { name: trimmedName } },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.success("Category updated.");
          onOpenChange(false);
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          const message = error?.response?.data?.error || error?.response?.data?.detail || "Failed to update category.";
          toast.error(message);
        },
      },
    );
  };

  if (!category) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/70 p-4">
      <div className="w-[440px] rounded-xl bg-white p-8 shadow-2xl">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800">Edit Category</h2>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input
            value={name}
            onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
            className="h-12 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
          />

          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 py-3 font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 py-3 font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              disabled={updateMutation.isPending}
            >
              {updateMutation.isPending ? "Saving..." : "Save"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useUpdateSkill } from "../categoryMutations.js";

export default function EditSkillModal({ open, onOpenChange, skill, categories = [] }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(skill?.name ?? "");
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState(skill?.categoryId ?? skill?.category ?? "");
  const updateSkillMutation = useUpdateSkill();

  if (!open || !skill) return null;

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const trimmedName = name.trim();

    if (!trimmedName || !categoryId) {
      toast.error("Please fill in all fields.");
      return;
    }

    updateSkillMutation.mutate(
      {
        id: skill.id,
        data: {
          name: trimmedName,
          categoryId: Number(categoryId),
          isCustom: Boolean(skill.isCustom),
        },
      },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.success("Skill updated.");
          onOpenChange(false);
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          const message = error?.response?.data?.error || error?.response?.data?.detail || "Failed to update skill.";
          toast.error(message);
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const handleCancel = () => {
    onOpenChange(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-[448px] rounded-xl bg-white shadow-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-8 pb-6 pt-8">
          <h2 className="text-[17px] font-semibold text-slate-800">Edit Skill</h2>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleCancel} className="text-slate-400 transition hover:text-slate-600">
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-8 pb-8">
          <p className="mb-8 text-xs text-slate-600">Update the skill details below.</p>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Skill Name</label>
              <input
                value={name}
                onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
                className="h-[50px] w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Category</label>
              <select
                value={categoryId}
                onChange={(event) => setCategoryId(event.target.value)}
                className="h-12 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              >
                <option value="">Select category</option>
                {categories.map((category) => (
                  <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>
                    {category.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCancel}
                className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 py-3 font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={updateSkillMutation.isPending}
                className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 py-3 font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {updateSkillMutation.isPending ? "Saving..." : "Save Changes"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useAiWorkflows } from "../../../aiworkflow/aiWorkflowQueries.js";
import { useNotificationSocket } from "../../../../hooks/notification/useNotificationSocket.jsx";

export default function AdminHeader() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const { data, isLoading } = useAiWorkflows({ status: "PendingApproval", page: 1, pageSize: 1 });
  const refresh = () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["ai-workflows"] });
  useNotificationSocket(() => refresh());
  const count = data?.count ?? data?.totalCount ?? (data?.results?.length || 0);
  return <header className="admin-header"><strong>Admin workspace</strong><a href="/admin/ai-workflows" className="admin-approval-badge">{isLoading ? "…" : count} pending</a></header>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useDashboardStats, useProposalsChart, useRevenueChart } from "../dashboardQueries.js";
import { usePendingPayouts } from "../../payoutmanagement/payoutQueries.js";
import PayoutReleaseModal from "../../payoutmanagement/PayoutReleaseModal.jsx";
import AdminStatsCard from "./AdminStatsCard.jsx";

const usd = (value) => new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: "USD" }).format(Number(value ?? 0));

function ChartRows({ rows, kind, loading }) {
  if (loading) return <div className="admin-loading">Loading chart data...</div>;
  if (!rows.length) return <div className="admin-empty">No chart data is available yet.</div>;
  return <div className="admin-chart-list">{rows.slice(-7).map((row) => <div key={row.date}><span>{new Date(row.date).toLocaleDateString()}</span><strong>{kind === "revenue" ? usd(row.revenueUsd) : `${row.submitted} submitted`}</strong><small>{kind === "revenue" ? `${row.totalTransactions} transactions` : `${row.accepted} accepted · ${row.rejected} rejected`}</small></div>)}</div>;
}

export default function AdminOverview() {
  const [selectedPayout, setSelectedPayout] = useState(null);
  const statsQuery = useDashboardStats();
  const payoutQuery = usePendingPayouts();
  const revenueQuery = useRevenueChart({ days: 30 });
  const proposalsQuery = useProposalsChart({ days: 30 });

  if (statsQuery.isLoading) return <section className="admin-page"><div className="admin-loading">Loading platform overview...</div></section>;
  if (statsQuery.isError) return <section className="admin-page"><div className="admin-form-error">Unable to load dashboard metrics.</div></section>;

  const stats = statsQuery.data ?? {};
  const payouts = Array.isArray(payoutQuery.data) ? payoutQuery.data : payoutQuery.data?.items ?? payoutQuery.data?.results ?? [];
  const revenue = Array.isArray(revenueQuery.data) ? revenueQuery.data : revenueQuery.data?.items ?? [];
  const proposals = Array.isArray(proposalsQuery.data) ? proposalsQuery.data : proposalsQuery.data?.items ?? [];

  return <section className="admin-page"><div className="admin-page__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Operations</span><h1>Platform overview</h1><p>Keep a clear view of activity, revenue, and obligations.</p></div></div><div className="admin-stats-grid"><AdminStatsCard label="Revenue" value={usd(stats.totalRevenueUsd)} detail={`${stats.totalTransactions ?? 0} transactions`} /><AdminStatsCard label="Platform fees" value={usd(stats.totalPlatformFeesUsd)} /><AdminStatsCard label="Pending payouts" value={usd(stats.totalPendingPayoutsUsd)} detail={`${stats.pendingPayoutCount ?? 0} awaiting release`} /><AdminStatsCard label="Active users" value={stats.activeUsers ?? 0} detail={`${stats.totalUsers ?? 0} total accounts`} /><AdminStatsCard label="Open projects" value={stats.openProjects ?? 0} detail={`${stats.totalProjects ?? 0} total projects`} /><AdminStatsCard label="Completed contracts" value={stats.completedContracts ?? 0} /></div><div className="admin-data-grid"><section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Revenue trend</span><h2>Last 30 days</h2></div></div><ChartRows rows={revenue} kind="revenue" loading={revenueQuery.isLoading} /></section><section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Proposal activity</span><h2>Last 30 days</h2></div></div><ChartRows rows={proposals} kind="proposals" loading={proposalsQuery.isLoading} /></section></div><section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Money movement</span><h2>Pending payouts</h2></div><span>{payouts.length} awaiting review</span></div>{payoutQuery.isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading pending payouts...</div>}{payoutQuery.isError && <div className="admin-form-error">Unable to load pending payouts.</div>}{!payoutQuery.isLoading && !payoutQuery.isError && payouts.length === 0 && <div className="admin-empty">No payouts are currently waiting for release.</div>}{payouts.length > 0 && <div className="admin-payout-list">{payouts.map((payout) => <div className="admin-payout-row" key={payout.id}><div><strong>Payment #{payout.id}</strong><small>{payout.status} · {new Date(payout.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</small></div><strong>{usd(payout.amount)}</strong><button className="button button--quiet" type="button" onClick={() => setSelectedPayout(payout)}>Review</button></div>)}</div>}</section><PayoutReleaseModal payment={selectedPayout} onClose={() => setSelectedPayout(null)} /></section>;
}

CONTENT:
export function getAdminErrorMessage(error) {
  if (error?.response?.data?.detail) return error.response.data.detail;
  if (error?.response?.data?.error) return error.response.data.error;
  if (error?.code === "ERR_NETWORK" || error?.message === "Network Error") return "Unable to reach the server. Check your connection.";
  return "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\admin\disputemanagement\DisputeDetail.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAdminDisputeDetail } from "./disputeQueries.js";
import { useResolveDispute } from "./disputeMutations.js";
import { useRefundPayment } from "../payoutmanagement/payoutMutations.js";

export default function DisputeDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data: dispute, isLoading, isError } = useAdminDisputeDetail(id);
  const resolve = useResolveDispute();
  const refund = useRefundPayment();
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  if (isLoading) return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-loading">Loading dispute case...</div></section>;
  if (isError || !dispute) return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">Case record unavailable.</div><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => navigate("/admin/disputes")}>Back to disputes</button></section>;
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Dispute #{dispute.id}</span><h1>{dispute.reason}</h1><p>{dispute.description}</p><p>Status: <strong>{dispute.status}</strong></p><p>Opened by: {dispute.openerRole || "Unknown"}</p>{dispute.evidenceUrls?.length > 0 && <ul>{dispute.evidenceUrls.map((url) => <li key={url}><a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Evidence</a></li>)}</ul>}{dispute.paymentId && <button className="button button--quiet" disabled={refund.isPending} onClick={() => refund.mutate(dispute.paymentId)}>Refund payment</button>}{dispute.status === "Pending" || dispute.status === "UnderReview" ? <form className="form-card" onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); resolve.mutate({ id: Number(id), status: "Resolved", adminNotes: notes }); }}><label>Admin notes<textarea required value={notes} onChange={(event) => setNotes(event.target.value)} /></label><div className="button-row"><button className="button button--quiet" type="button" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: Number(id), status: "Rejected", adminNotes: notes })}>Reject dispute</button><button className="button button--accent" disabled={resolve.isPending}>Resolve dispute</button></div></form> : <p>{dispute.adminNotes || "No resolution notes."}</p>}</section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useAdminDisputes } from "./disputeQueries.js";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function DisputeList() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useAdminDisputes(status ? { status } : {});
  const disputes = data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Trust & safety</span><h1>Dispute resolution center</h1></div><span>{data?.count ?? disputes.length} cases</span></div><div className="button-row">{["", "Pending", "UnderReview", "Resolved", "Rejected"].map((item) => <button key={item || "all"} className={`button ${status === item ? "button--accent" : "button--quiet"}`} onClick={() => setStatus(item)}>{item || "All"}</button>)}</div>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading disputes...</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">Unable to load disputes. Please try again.</div>}{!isLoading && !isError && disputes.length === 0 && <div className="admin-empty">No disputes match the selected filter.</div>}<div className="admin-payout-list">{disputes.map((dispute) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={dispute.id}><div><strong>Dispute #{dispute.id}</strong><small>{dispute.reason} · {dispute.openerRole || "Unknown opener"}</small></div><div><strong>{dispute.status}</strong><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => navigate(`/admin/disputes/${dispute.id}`)}>View case</button></div></article>)}</div></section>;
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\admin\disputemanagement\__tests__\adminErrorState.test.js
CONTENT:
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { getAdminErrorMessage } from "../adminErrorState.js";

describe("admin contract operation errors", () => {
  it("returns a safe message for a network error", () => {
    expect(getAdminErrorMessage({ code: "ERR_NETWORK" })).toBe("Unable to reach the server. Check your connection.");
  });

  it("prefers a server detail message", () => {
    expect(getAdminErrorMessage({ response: { data: { detail: "Dispute was already resolved." } } })).toBe("Dispute was already resolved.");
  });

  it("falls back to a generic message", () => {
    expect(getAdminErrorMessage({})).toBe("Something went wrong. Please try again.");
  });
});

export const fetchAdminFAQs = async (params = {}) => (await axiosInstance.get("/content/admin/faq", { params })).data;
export const createFAQ = async (payload) => (await axiosInstance.post("/content/admin/faq", payload)).data;
export const updateFAQ = async ({ id, ...payload }) => (await axiosInstance.put(`/content/admin/faq/${id}`, payload)).data;
export const deleteFAQ = async (id) => (await axiosInstance.delete(`/content/admin/faq/${id}`)).data;

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\admin\faq\FAQManagement.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAdminFAQs } from "./faqQueries.js";
import { useFAQMutations } from "./faqMutations.js";

export default function FAQManagement() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useAdminFAQs();
  const { create, update, remove } = useFAQMutations();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ question: "", answer: "", category: "General", sortOrder: 0 });
  const rows = data?.items || data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  const submit = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); create.mutate(form, { onSuccess: () => setForm({ question: "", answer: "", category: "General", sortOrder: 0 }) }); };
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><h1>FAQ management</h1>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading FAQ entries…</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error">Unable to load FAQ entries.</div>}<form className="form-card" onSubmit={submit}><h2>Add FAQ</h2><input required placeholder="Question" value={form.question} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, question: e.target.value })} /><textarea required placeholder="Answer" value={form.answer} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, answer: e.target.value })} /><select value={form.category} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, category: e.target.value })}>{["General", "Account", "Projects", "Payments"].map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}</select><button className="button button--accent" disabled={create.isPending}>Add FAQ</button></form><div className="admin-payout-list">{rows.map((row) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={row.id}><div><strong>{row.question}</strong><small>{row.answer}</small></div><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => remove.mutate(row.id)} disabled={remove.isPending}>Delete</button></article>)}</div></section>;
}

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createFAQ, deleteFAQ, updateFAQ } from "./faqApi.js";
export const useFAQMutations = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); const refresh = () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-faqs"] }); return { create: useMutation({ mutationFn: createFAQ, onSuccess: refresh }), update: useMutation({ mutationFn: updateFAQ, onSuccess: refresh }), remove: useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteFAQ, onSuccess: refresh }) }; };
export const useCreateFAQ = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: createFAQ, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-faqs"] }) }); };
export const useUpdateFAQ = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: updateFAQ, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-faqs"] }) }); };
export const useDeleteFAQ = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteFAQ, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-faqs"] }) }); };

---END FILE---

import { useState } from "react";
import { usePendingPayouts } from "./payoutQueries.js";
import PayoutReleaseModal from "./PayoutReleaseModal.jsx";
import { useRefundPayment } from "./payoutMutations.js";
import formatMoney from "../../../utils/formatMoney.js";

export default function PaymentRelease() {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = usePendingPayouts({ page, pageSize: 20 });
  const refund = useRefundPayment();
  const payments = data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Money movement</span><h1>Pending payouts</h1></div><span>{data?.count ?? payments.length} awaiting review</span></div>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading payouts...</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">Unable to load payouts.</div>}{!isLoading && !isError && payments.length === 0 && <div className="admin-empty">No payouts are ready for release.</div>}<div className="admin-payout-list">{payments.map((payment) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={payment.id}><div><strong>Payment #{payment.id}</strong><small>{payment.status} · {payment.disputeStatus || "No dispute"}</small></div><strong>{formatMoney(payment.freelancerAmount ?? payment.amount)}</strong><div className="button-row"><button className="button button--accent" onClick={() => setSelected(payment)}>Release</button><button className="button button--quiet" disabled={refund.isPending} onClick={() => refund.mutate(payment.id)}>Refund</button></div></article>)}</div><div className="button-row"><button className="button button--quiet" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value - 1)}>Previous</button><span>Page {page}</span><button className="button button--quiet" disabled={!data?.totalPages || page >= data.totalPages} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value + 1)}>Next</button></div><PayoutReleaseModal payment={selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} /></section>;
}

import { useReleasePayout } from "./payoutMutations.js";

export default function PayoutReleaseModal({ payment, onClose }) {
  const mutation = useReleasePayout();
  if (!payment) return null;
  const confirm = () => mutation.mutate(payment.id, { onSuccess: onClose });
  return <div className="admin-modal-backdrop" role="presentation" onMouseDown={onClose}><section className="admin-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="payout-title" onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}><button className="admin-modal__close" type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">X</button><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Payout approval</span><h2 id="payout-title">Release payout?</h2><p>Release {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: "USD" }).format(Number(payment.amount ?? 0))} for payment #{payment.id}.</p>{mutation.isError && <div className="admin-form-error">The payout could not be released.</div>}<div className="admin-modal__actions"><button className="button button--quiet" type="button" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button><button className="button button--accent" type="button" disabled={mutation.isPending} onClick={confirm}>{mutation.isPending ? "Releasing..." : "Release payout"}</button></div></section></div>;
}

export default function PrivacyPolicy() {

  const [form, setForm] = useState({ heading: "", description: "", sortOrder: 0 });
