import { useState } from "react";
import { useAdminReviews } from "./reviewQueries.js";
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { deleteReview } from "./reviewApi.js";

export default function ReviewList() {
  const [type, setType] = useState("");
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useAdminReviews(type ? { type } : {});
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const remove = useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteReview, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-reviews"] }) });
  const reviews = data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Trust & safety</span><h1>Review management</h1></div><span>{reviews.length} reviews</span></div><div className="button-row">{["", "Freelancer", "Client"].map((item) => <button key={item || "all"} className={`button ${type === item ? "button--accent" : "button--quiet"}`} onClick={() => setType(item)}>{item || "All"}</button>)}</div>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading reviews...</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">Unable to load reviews.</div>}{!isLoading && !isError && reviews.length === 0 && <div className="admin-empty">No reviews found.</div>}<div className="admin-payout-list">{reviews.map((review) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={review.id}><div><strong>{review.reviewerName || review.reviewerId}</strong><small>{review.comment}</small></div><div><strong>{review.rating} / 5</strong><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => remove.mutate(review.id)} disabled={remove.isPending}>Delete</button></div></article>)}</div></section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useGlobalSettings } from "./adminSettingsQueries.js";
import { useUpdateSettings } from "./adminsettingsMutations.js";

export default function AdminSettings() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useGlobalSettings();
  const update = useUpdateSettings();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ SupportEmail: "", CommissionPercentage: 0, PayPalEmail: "", MinBriefConfidence: 0, ProposalScreeningThreshold: 0, MatchScoreThreshold: 0, DefaultCurrency: "USD" });
  const set = (key, value) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }));
  const load = () => data && setForm({ SupportEmail: data.supportEmail ?? "", CommissionPercentage: data.commissionPercentage ?? 0, PayPalEmail: data.payPalEmail ?? "", MinBriefConfidence: data.minBriefConfidence ?? 0, ProposalScreeningThreshold: data.proposalScreeningThreshold ?? 0, MatchScoreThreshold: data.matchScoreThreshold ?? 0, DefaultCurrency: data.defaultCurrency ?? "USD" });
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><h1>Platform settings</h1>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading settings…</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error">Unable to load settings.</div>}<form className="form-card" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); update.mutate(form); }}><label>Support email<input value={form.SupportEmail} onChange={(e) => set("SupportEmail", e.target.value)} /></label><label>Commission percentage<input type="number" min="0" max="100" step="0.01" value={form.CommissionPercentage} onChange={(e) => set("CommissionPercentage", e.target.value)} /></label><label>PayPal email<input value={form.PayPalEmail} onChange={(e) => set("PayPalEmail", e.target.value)} /></label><label>Default currency<input value={form.DefaultCurrency} onChange={(e) => set("DefaultCurrency", e.target.value)} /></label><button className="button button--accent" disabled={update.isPending}>Save settings</button><button type="button" className="button button--quiet" onClick={load}>Load current values</button></form></section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useTicketDetails } from "./supportQueries.js";
import { useReplyMutation } from "./supportMutations.js";

export default function SupportTicketDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data: ticket, isLoading, isError } = useTicketDetails(id);
  const reply = useReplyMutation();
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  if (isLoading) return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-loading">Loading ticket…</div></section>;
  if (isError || !ticket) return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-form-error">Ticket unavailable.</div></section>;
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => navigate("/admin/support")}>Back to tickets</button><h1>{ticket.subject}</h1><p>{ticket.message}</p><p>Status: <strong>{ticket.status}</strong></p>{ticket.adminReply && <div className="form-card"><strong>Admin reply</strong><p>{ticket.adminReply}</p></div>}<form className="form-card" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); reply.mutate({ id: Number(id), reply: message }, { onSuccess: () => setMessage("") }); }}><h2>Reply to user</h2><textarea required value={message} onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)} /><button className="button button--accent" disabled={reply.isPending}>Send reply</button></form></section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useTicketDetails, useSupportTickets } from "./supportQueries.js";
import { useReplyMutation } from "./supportMutations.js";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function SupportTicketList() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useSupportTickets(status ? { status } : {});
  const rows = data?.items || data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Trust & safety</span><h1>Support tickets</h1></div><span>{data?.totalCount ?? rows.length} tickets</span></div><select className="input" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)}><option value="">All statuses</option>{["Open", "Resolved", "Closed"].map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}</select>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading support tickets…</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error">Unable to load support tickets.</div>}{!isLoading && !isError && rows.length === 0 && <div className="admin-empty">No support tickets found.</div>}<div className="admin-payout-list">{rows.map((ticket) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={ticket.id}><div><strong>{ticket.subject}</strong><small>{ticket.userEmail || "User"} · {ticket.status}</small></div><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => navigate(`/admin/support/${ticket.id}`)}>View ticket</button></article>)}</div></section>;

export const useTermsMutations = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); const refresh = () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-terms"] }); return { create: useMutation({ mutationFn: createTermsSection, onSuccess: refresh }), update: useMutation({ mutationFn: updateTermsSection, onSuccess: refresh }), remove: useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteTermsSection, onSuccess: refresh }) }; };

import { useState } from "react";
import { useTermsSections } from "./termsQueries.js";
import { useTermsMutations } from "./temsMutations.js";

export default function TermAndConditions() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useTermsSections();
  const { create, update, remove } = useTermsMutations();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ heading: "", description: "", sortOrder: 0 });
  const rows = data?.items || data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  const submit = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); create.mutate(form, { onSuccess: () => setForm({ heading: "", description: "", sortOrder: 0 }) }); };
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><h1>Terms management</h1>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading terms…</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error">Unable to load terms.</div>}<form className="form-card" onSubmit={submit}><h2>Add terms section</h2><input required placeholder="Heading" value={form.heading} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, heading: e.target.value })} /><textarea required placeholder="Description" value={form.description} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })} /><button className="button button--accent" disabled={create.isPending}>Add section</button></form><div className="admin-payout-list">{rows.map((row) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={row.id}><div><strong>{row.heading}</strong><small>{row.description}</small></div><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => remove.mutate(row.id)}>Delete</button></article>)}</div></section>;
}

export default function AdminTransactions() {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = usePlatformTransactions({ page, pageSize: 20, status: status || undefined });
  const transactions = data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-subsection__heading"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Platform finance</span><h1>Transactions</h1></div><span>{data?.count ?? transactions.length} payments</span></div><select className="input" value={status} onChange={(event) => { setStatus(event.target.value); setPage(1); }}><option value="">All statuses</option>{["Pending", "Escrow", "Released", "Refunded"].map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}</select>{isLoading && <div className="admin-loading">Loading transactions...</div>}{isError && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">Unable to load transactions.</div>}{!isLoading && !isError && transactions.length === 0 && <div className="admin-empty">No transactions found.</div>}<div className="admin-payout-list">{transactions.map((transaction) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={transaction.id}><div><strong>Payment #{transaction.id}</strong><small>{transaction.gateway || "PayPal"} · {transaction.status}</small></div><div><strong>{formatMoney(transaction.amount)}</strong><small>Fee {formatMoney(transaction.platformFee)}</small></div></article>)}</div><div className="button-row"><button className="button button--quiet" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value - 1)}>Previous</button><span>Page {page}</span><button className="button button--quiet" disabled={!data?.totalPages || page >= data.totalPages} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value + 1)}>Next</button></div></section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useSoftDeleteUser, useToggleUserActive } from "./usersMutations.js";
import ViewUserModal from "./components/ViewUserModal.jsx";

import DeleteUserModal from "./components/DeleteUserModal.jsx";

export default function UserTable({ users }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
  const toggle = useToggleUserActive();
  const remove = useSoftDeleteUser();

  return <><div className="admin-table-wrap"><table className="admin-table"><thead><tr><th>User</th><th>Role</th><th>Status</th><th>Joined</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>{users.map((user) => <tr key={user.id}><td><button className="admin-table__user" type="button" onClick={() => { setSelected(user); setModal("view"); }}><span className="admin-user-avatar">{user.fullName?.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}</span><span><strong>{user.fullName}</strong><small>{user.email}</small></span></button></td><td>{user.role}</td><td><span className={user.isActive ? "Block" : "Activate"}</button><button type="button" onClick={() => { setSelected(user); setModal("delete"); }}>Archive</button></div>}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table>{users.length === 0 && <div className="admin-empty">No users match the current filters.</div>}</div><ViewUserModal user={modal === "view" ? selected : null} onClose={() => setModal(null)} /><SuspendUserModal user={modal === "suspend" ? selected : null} onClose={() => setModal(null)} pending={toggle.isPending} error={toggle.error} onConfirm={() => toggle.mutate(selected.id, { onSuccess: () => setModal(null) })} /><DeleteUserModal user={modal === "delete" ? selected : null} onClose={() => setModal(null)} pending={remove.isPending} error={remove.error} onConfirm={() => remove.mutate(selected.id, { onSuccess: () => setModal(null) })} /></>;
}

export default function DeleteUserModal({ user, onClose, onConfirm, pending, error }) {
  if (!user) return null;
  return <div className="admin-modal-backdrop" role="presentation" onMouseDown={onClose}><section className="admin-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}><button className="admin-modal__close" type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">X</button><h2>Archive user?</h2><p>{user.fullName} will be soft-deleted and removed from normal admin listings.</p>{error && <div className="admin-form-error">Unable to archive this user.</div>}<div className="admin-modal__actions"><button className="button button--quiet" type="button" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button><button className="button button--danger" type="button" disabled={pending} onClick={onConfirm}>{pending ? "Archiving..." : "Archive user"}</button></div></section></div>;
}

export default function SuspendUserModal({ user, onClose, onConfirm, pending, error }) {
  if (!user) return null;
  const action = user.isActive ? "block" : "reactivate";
  return <div className="admin-modal-backdrop" role="presentation" onMouseDown={onClose}><section className="admin-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}><button className="admin-modal__close" type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">X</button><h2>{user.isActive ? "Block user?" : "Reactivate user?"}</h2><p>This will {action} access for {user.fullName}.</p>{error && <div className="admin-form-error">Unable to update this user.</div>}<div className="admin-modal__actions"><button className="button button--quiet" type="button" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button><button className="button button--accent" type="button" disabled={pending} onClick={onConfirm}>{pending ? "Saving..." : user.isActive ? "Block user" : "Reactivate"}</button></div></section></div>;
}

import { useParams, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAiWorkflow } from "./aiWorkflowQueries.js";
import { useCancelAiWorkflow } from "./aiWorkflowMutations.js";
import WorkflowApprovalPanel from "./WorkflowApprovalPanel.jsx";

export default function AiWorkflowDetail() {  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data: workflow, isLoading, isError } = useAiWorkflow(id);
  const cancel = useCancelAiWorkflow();
  if (isLoading) return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-loading">Loading workflow…</div></section>;
  if (isError || !workflow) return <section className="admin-subsection"><div className="admin-form-error">Workflow unavailable.</div></section>;
  return <section className="admin-subsection"><button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => navigate("/admin/ai-workflows")}>Back to monitor</button><h1>Workflow #{workflow.id}</h1><p>{workflow.objective}</p><p>Status: <strong>{workflow.status}</strong></p><h2>Step log</h2><div className="admin-payout-list">{(workflow.stepLogs || workflow.steps || []).map((step) => <article className="admin-payout-row" key={step.id}><div><strong>{step.stepName}</strong><small>{step.status} · {step.durationMs ?? 0}ms</small></div><span>{step.output}</span></article>)}</div><WorkflowApprovalPanel workflow={workflow} />{["Pending", "Running", "PendingApproval"].includes(String(workflow.status)) && <button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => cancel.mutate(Number(id))} disabled={cancel.isPending}>Cancel workflow</button>}</section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { workflowState } from "./workflowDisplay.js";
import { useApproveAiWorkflow } from "./aiWorkflowMutations.js";

export default function WorkflowApprovalPanel({ workflow }) {
  const [decision, setDecision] = useState("approve");
  const [rationale, setRationale] = useState("");
  const mutation = useApproveAiWorkflow();
  const state = workflowState(workflow);
  const submit = (event) => { event.preventDefault(); mutation.mutate({ id: workflow.id, decision, rationale }); };
  return state.canApprove ? <form className="form-card" onSubmit={submit}><h2>Approval decision</h2><label>Rationale<textarea required value={rationale} onChange={(e) => setRationale(e.target.value)} /></label><div className="button-row">{["approve", "reject", "request_revision"].map((item) => <button type="button" key={item} className={`button ${decision === item ? "button--accent" : "button--quiet"}`} onClick={() => setDecision(item)}>{item.replace("_", " ")}</button>)}</div><button className="button button--accent" disabled={mutation.isPending}>Submit decision</button></form> : null;
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { workflowRows, workflowState } from "../workflowDisplay.js";

describe("AI workflow monitor display", () => {
  it("normalizes paginated and legacy workflow responses", () => {
    expect(workflowRows({ results: [{ id: 1 }] })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(workflowRows([{ id: 2 }])).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(workflowRows(null)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("marks approval states correctly", () => {
    expect(workflowState({ status: "PendingApproval" }).canApprove).toBe(true);
    expect(workflowState({ status: "Completed" }).canApprove).toBe(false);
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { workflowState } from "../workflowDisplay.js";

describe("AI workflow approval panel decisions", () => {
  it("offers approval actions for pending approval", () => {
    const state = workflowState({ status: "PendingApproval" });
    expect(state.canApprove).toBe(true);
    expect(state.status).toBe("PendingApproval");
  });
  it("hides approval actions for completed workflows", () => {
    expect(workflowState({ status: "Completed" }).canApprove).toBe(false);

  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) {
    return null;
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (!otp.trim()) {
      setError("Enter the verification code.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);

    try {
      const response = await verifyOtp({ email, otp, password, fullName, role });
      dispatch(setCredentials({
        accessToken: response.accessToken ?? response.AccessToken,
        user: {
          id: response.userId ?? response.UserId,
          email: response.email ?? response.Email,
          fullName: response.fullName ?? response.FullName,
          role: response.role ?? response.Role,
        },
      }));
      onSuccess?.(response);
      onClose?.();
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.response?.data?.detail ?? "That verification code was not accepted.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.65)", display: "grid", placeItems: "center", padding: 20 }}>
      <div style={{ width: "min(420px, 100%)", background: "#fff", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, boxShadow: "0 20px 60px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18)" }}>
        <h3 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Verify your account</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <InputBox label="Verification code" name="otp" value={otp} onChange={(event) => setOtp(event.target.value)} inputMode="numeric" autoComplete="one-time-code" />
          {error && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 12, marginTop: 18 }}>
            <button type="button" className="button button--secondary" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" className="button button--accent" disabled={pending}>{pending ? "Verifying..." : "Verify"}</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\account\auth\authslice.js
CONTENT:
import { createSlice } from "@reduxjs/toolkit";
import {
  fetchUser,
  loginThunk,
  logoutThunk,
  refreshSession,
} from "./authThunks.js";

const initialState = {
  user: null,
  accessToken: null,
  loading: false,
  initialized: false,
};

const authSlice = createSlice({
  name: "auth",
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    setCredentials: (state, action) => {
      state.user = action.payload.user ?? state.user;
      state.accessToken = action.payload.accessToken ?? state.accessToken;
      state.initialized = true;
      state.loading = false;
    },
    logout: () => ({ ...initialState, initialized: true }),
    finishLoading: (state) => {
      state.loading = false;
      state.initialized = true;
    },
    setUser: (state, action) => {
      state.user = action.payload;
    },
  },
  extraReducers: (builder) => {
    builder
      .addCase(loginThunk.pending, (state) => {
        state.loading = true;
      })
      .addCase(loginThunk.fulfilled, (state, action) => {
        state.user = action.payload.user;
        state.accessToken = action.payload.accessToken;
        state.loading = false;
        state.initialized = true;
      })
      .addCase(loginThunk.rejected, (state) => {
        state.loading = false;
        state.initialized = true;
      })
      .addCase(fetchUser.pending, (state) => {
        state.loading = true;
      })
      .addCase(fetchUser.fulfilled, (state, action) => {
        state.user = action.payload;
        state.loading = false;
        state.initialized = true;
      })
      .addCase(fetchUser.rejected, (state) => {
        state.loading = false;
        state.initialized = true;
      })
      .addCase(logoutThunk.fulfilled, () => ({ ...initialState, initialized: true }))
      .addCase(refreshSession.fulfilled, (state, action) => {
        state.accessToken = action.payload.accessToken;
        state.initialized = true;
      })
      .addCase(refreshSession.rejected, () => ({ ...initialState, initialized: true }));
  },
});

export const { finishLoading, logout, setCredentials, setUser } = authSlice.actions;
export default authSlice.reducer;

---END FILE---

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\account\auth\authThunks.js
CONTENT:
import { createAsyncThunk } from "@reduxjs/toolkit";
import * as authApi from "../../../../api/auth/authApi.js";

const apiError = (error) =>
  error.response?.data ?? { error: error.message ?? "Request failed" };

export const loginThunk = createAsyncThunk(
  "auth/login",
  async (credentials, thunkApi) => {
    try {
      return await authApi.loginUser(credentials);
    } catch (error) {
      return thunkApi.rejectWithValue(apiError(error));
    }
  },
);

export const fetchUser = createAsyncThunk(
  "auth/fetchUser",
  async (_, thunkApi) => {
    try {
      const response = await authApi.getCurrentUser();
      return response.user ?? response;
    } catch (error) {
      return thunkApi.rejectWithValue(apiError(error));
    }
  },
);

export const logoutThunk = createAsyncThunk(
  "auth/logout",
  async (_, thunkApi) => {
    try {
      return await authApi.logoutUser();
    } catch (error) {
      return thunkApi.rejectWithValue(apiError(error));
    }
  },
);

export const refreshSession = createAsyncThunk(
  "auth/refresh",
  async (_, thunkApi) => {
    try {
      return await authApi.refreshToken();
    } catch (error) {
      return thunkApi.rejectWithValue(apiError(error));
    }
  },
);

import { useModal } from "../../../../hooks/modal/useModalStore.jsx";
import SignInModal from "../SignInModal.jsx";
import SignupModal from "../SignupModal.jsx";
import ContractOfferModal from "../../contract/components/ContractOfferModal.jsx";
import ApproveContractModal from "../../contract/components/ApproveContractModal.jsx";
import ClientDisputeModal from "../../contract/components/ClientDisputeModal.jsx";
import OfferModal from "../../../freelancer/offers/OfferModal.jsx";

export default function AuthModalHost() {
  const { modal, modalProps, closeModal } = useModal();
  if (modal === "signin") return <SignInModal onClose={closeModal} />;
  if (modal === "signup") return <SignupModal onClose={closeModal} />;
  if (modal === "contract-offer") return <ContractOfferModal {...modalProps} onClose={closeModal} />;
  if (modal === "approve-contract") return <ApproveContractModal {...modalProps} onClose={closeModal} />;
  if (modal === "client-dispute") return <ClientDisputeModal {...modalProps} onClose={closeModal} />;
  if (modal === "offer-modal") return <OfferModal {...modalProps} onClose={closeModal} />;
  return null;
}

CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { deleteAccount, requestDeleteOtp } from "../../../../api/auth/authApi.js";
import InputBox from "./InputBox.jsx";

export default function DeleteAccountModal({ isOpen, onClose }) {
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState("");
  const [confirmText, setConfirmText] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [otpRequested, setOtpRequested] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) {
    return null;
  }

  const handleRequestOtp = async () => {
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");
    setPending(true);

    try {
      await requestDeleteOtp();
      setOtpRequested(true);
      setSuccess("A one-time code has been sent to your email.");
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.response?.data?.error ?? "Unable to send the deletion code.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (!otp.trim()) {
      setError("Enter the one-time code sent to your email.");
      return;
    }

    if (confirmText.trim() !== "DELETE") {
      setError("Type DELETE to confirm account deletion.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);

    try {
      await deleteAccount({ otp });
      setSuccess("Account deleted successfully.");
      onClose();
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.response?.data?.error ?? "The account deletion request was rejected.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.65)", display: "grid", placeItems: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 40 }}>
      <div style={{ width: "min(520px, 100%)", background: "#fff", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, boxShadow: "0 20px 60px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18)" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: "#991b1b" }}>Delete account</h3>
          <button type="button" className="button button--secondary" onClick={onClose}>Close</button>
        </div>

        <p style={{ marginTop: 0, color: "#475569" }}>This action is permanent and cannot be undone.</p>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 12, marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <button type="button" className="button button--secondary" onClick={handleRequestOtp} disabled={pending || otpRequested}>
            {otpRequested ? "Code sent" : "Send delete code"}
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleDelete} noValidate>
          <InputBox label="One-time code" name="otp" value={otp} onChange={(event) => setOtp(event.target.value)} inputMode="numeric" />
          <InputBox label="Type DELETE to confirm" name="confirmText" value={confirmText} onChange={(event) => setConfirmText(event.target.value)} />
          {error && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
          {success && <div className="admin-form-success" role="status">{success}</div>}
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 12, marginTop: 18 }}>
            <button type="button" className="button button--secondary" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" className="button button--danger" disabled={pending}>{pending ? "Deleting..." : "Delete account"}</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { forgotPassword } from "../../../../api/auth/authApi.js";
import InputBox from "./InputBox.jsx";

export default function ForgotPasswordModal({ isOpen, initialEmail = "", onClose }) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(initialEmail);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) {
    return null;
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (!email.trim()) {
      setError("Enter the email connected to your account.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);

    try {
      await forgotPassword({ email });
      setSuccess("If the account exists, a reset link has been sent.");
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.response?.data?.error ?? "Unable to send the reset email.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.6)", display: "grid", placeItems: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 40 }}>
      <div style={{ width: "min(480px, 100%)", background: "#fff", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, boxShadow: "0 20px 60px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18)" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Forgot password</h3>
          <button type="button" className="button button--secondary" onClick={onClose}>Close</button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <InputBox label="Email" name="email" type="email" value={email} onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)} autoComplete="email" />
          {error && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
          {success && <div className="admin-form-success" role="status">{success}</div>}
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 12, marginTop: 18 }}>
            <button type="button" className="button button--secondary" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" className="button button--accent" disabled={pending}>{pending ? "Sending..." : "Send reset email"}</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useDispatch } from "react-redux";
import { loginUser } from "../../../../api/auth/authApi.js";
import { setCredentials } from "../auth/authslice.js";
import { signinSchema } from "../validation/authSchemas.js";
import InputBox from "./InputBox.jsx";

export default function SignInForm({ onClose }) {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const [values, setValues] = useState({ email: "", password: "" });
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const update = (event) => setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [event.target.name]: event.target.value }));
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const result = signinSchema.safeParse(values);
    if (!result.success) { setErrors(Object.fromEntries(result.error.issues.map((issue) => [issue.path[0], issue.message]))); return; }
    setErrors({}); setError(""); setPending(true);
    try { const response = await loginUser(values); dispatch(setCredentials({ accessToken: response.accessToken ?? response.AccessToken, user: { id: response.userId ?? response.UserId, email: response.email ?? response.Email, fullName: response.fullName ?? response.FullName, role: response.role ?? response.Role } })); onClose(); } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.response?.data?.detail ?? "Unable to sign in."); } finally { setPending(false); }
  };
  return <form className="auth-form" onSubmit={submit} noValidate><InputBox label="Email" name="email" type="email" value={values.email} onChange={update} autoComplete="email" error={errors.email} /><InputBox label="Password" name="password" type="password" value={values.password} onChange={update} autoComplete="current-password" error={errors.password} />{error && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}<button className="button button--accent" type="submit" disabled={pending}>{pending ? "Signing in..." : "Sign in"}</button></form>;
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\account\components\SignupForm.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { sendOtp } from "../../../../api/auth/authApi.js";
import { signupSchema } from "../validation/authSchemas.js";
import InputBox from "./InputBox.jsx";
import OtpModal from "../OtpModal.jsx";

export default function SignupForm({ onClose }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState({ fullName: "", email: "", password: "", confirmPassword: "", role: 1, otp: "" });
  const [stage, setStage] = useState("details");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const update = (event) => setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [event.target.name]: event.target.value }));
  const submitDetails = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const result = signupSchema.safeParse(values);
    if (!result.success) {
      setError(result.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Check your details.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      await sendOtp({ email: values.email });
      setStage("otp");
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.response?.data?.detail ?? "Unable to send verification code.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  if (stage === "otp") {
    return (
      <OtpModal
        isOpen={true}
        email={values.email}
        password={values.password}
        fullName={values.fullName}
        role={values.role}
        onClose={onClose}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <form className="auth-form" onSubmit={submitDetails} noValidate>
      <InputBox label="Full name" name="fullName" value={values.fullName} onChange={update} autoComplete="name" />
      <InputBox label="Email" name="email" type="email" value={values.email} onChange={update} autoComplete="email" />
      <InputBox label="Password" name="password" type="password" value={values.password} onChange={update} autoComplete="new-password" />
      <InputBox label="Confirm password" name="confirmPassword" type="password" value={values.confirmPassword} onChange={update} autoComplete="new-password" />
      {error && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
      <button className="button button--accent" type="submit" disabled={pending}>{pending ? "Sending code..." : "Continue"}</button>
    </form>
  );
}

import { googleAuth } from "../../../../api/auth/authApi.js";

export const handleGoogleSignup = async ({ idToken, role = 1 }) => {
  if (!idToken || !String(idToken).trim()) {
    throw new Error("Google authentication token is required.");
  }

  const response = await googleAuth({ idToken, role });
  return response;
};

export default handleGoogleSignup;

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\account\pages\ResetPasswordPage.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { resetPassword } from "../../../../api/auth/authApi.js";
import InputBox from "../components/InputBox.jsx";

const passwordRules = [
  "At least 8 characters",
  "One uppercase letter",
  "One lowercase letter",
  "One number",
  "One special character",
];

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [values, setValues] = useState({
    email: searchParams.get("email") ?? "",
    token: searchParams.get("token") ?? "",
    newPassword: "",
    confirmPassword: "",
  });
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  const update = (event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;
    setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (!values.email || !values.token) {
      setError("The password reset email and token are required.");
      return;
    }

    if (values.newPassword.length < 8) {
      setError("Password must be at least 8 characters long.");
      return;
    }

    if (values.newPassword !== values.confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);

    try {
      await resetPassword({ email: values.email, token: values.token, newPassword: values.newPassword });
      setSuccess("Your password has been reset successfully.");
      setValues({ email: values.email, token: values.token, newPassword: "", confirmPassword: "" });
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.response?.data?.error ?? "Unable to reset the password.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 560, margin: "48px auto", padding: "0 20px" }}>
      <div className="auth-shell">
        <h2>Reset password</h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <InputBox label="Email" name="email" type="email" value={values.email} onChange={update} autoComplete="email" />
          <InputBox label="Reset token" name="token" value={values.token} onChange={update} autoComplete="one-time-code" />
          <InputBox label="New password" name="newPassword" type="password" value={values.newPassword} onChange={update} autoComplete="new-password" />
          <InputBox label="Confirm password" name="confirmPassword" type="password" value={values.confirmPassword} onChange={update} autoComplete="new-password" />

          <ul style={{ margin: "0 0 18px", paddingLeft: 18, color: "#475569" }}>
            {passwordRules.map((rule) => <li key={rule}>{rule}</li>)}
          </ul>

          {error && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
          {success && <div className="admin-form-success" role="status">{success}</div>}

          <button type="submit" className="button button--accent" disabled={pending}>
            {pending ? "Resetting..." : "Reset password"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

---END FILE---

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\account\validation\authSchemas.js
CONTENT:
import { z } from "zod";

const passwordSchema = z
  .string()
  .min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters")
  .regex(/[A-Z]/, "Password must contain an uppercase letter")
  .regex(/[a-z]/, "Password must contain a lowercase letter")
  .regex(/[0-9]/, "Password must contain a number")
  .regex(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/, "Password must contain a special character");

export const signupSchema = z
  .object({
    fullName: z.string().trim().min(3).max(200),
    email: z.string().trim().email(),
    password: passwordSchema,
    confirmPassword: z.string(),
    role: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(2),
  })
  .refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
    path: ["confirmPassword"],
    message: "Passwords do not match",
  });

export const signinSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().trim().email("Enter a valid email address"),
  password: z.string().min(1, "Password is required"),
});

---END FILE---

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\account\__tests__\authSchemas.test.js
CONTENT:
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { signinSchema } from "../validation/authSchemas.js";

describe("signinSchema", () => {
  it("accepts valid credentials", () => {
    const result = signinSchema.safeParse({
      email: "user@example.com",
      password: "Str0ng!Pass",
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects invalid email", () => {
    const result = signinSchema.safeParse({
      email: "not-an-email",
      password: "Str0ng!Pass",
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error.issues[0].path).toEqual(["email"]);
  });

  it("rejects empty password", () => {
    const result = signinSchema.safeParse({
      email: "user@example.com",
      password: "",
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error.issues[0].path).toEqual(["password"]);
  });
});

import axiosInstance from "../../../api/axiosInstance.js";

export const fetchContracts = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/contracts")).data;
export const fetchContract = async (id) => (await axiosInstance.get(`/contracts/${id}`)).data;
export const fetchContractOffers = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/contracts/offers")).data;
export const acceptContract = async ({ contractId, signature, signatureType = "text" }) =>
  (await axiosInstance.post(`/contracts/${contractId}/accept`, { signature, signatureType })).data;
export const requestContractRevision = async ({ contractId, reason }) =>
  (await axiosInstance.post(`/revisions/contract/${contractId}`, { reason })).data;
export const updateContractStatus = async ({ contractId, status }) =>
  (await axiosInstance.patch(`/contracts/${contractId}/status`, { status })).data;
export const verifyPayment = async (payload) => (await axiosInstance.post("/payments/verify", payload)).data;
export const createReview = async (payload) => (await axiosInstance.post("/reviews", payload)).data;

import { useState } from "react";
import { useContract } from "./contractQueries";
import { useAcceptContract, useRequestRevision, useUpdateContractStatus } from "./contractMutations";
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useModal } from "../../../hooks/modal/useModalStore.jsx";
import ContractOfferModal from "./components/ContractOfferModal.jsx";
import formatMoney from "../../../utils/formatMoney";

export default function ContractDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { data: contract, isLoading, isError } = useContract(id);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const accept = useAcceptContract();
  const revision = useRequestRevision();
  const status = useUpdateContractStatus();
  const { openModal } = useModal();
  if (isLoading) return <section className="content-section"><p>Loading contract…</p></section>;
  if (isError || !contract) return <section className="content-section"><p role="alert">Unable to load this contract.</p></section>;
  const value = (a, b) => contract[a] ?? contract[b];
  return <section className="content-section"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Contract #{id}</span><h1>{value("projectTitle", "project_title") || "Contract detail"}</h1><p>{value("freelancerName", "freelancer_name")} · {value("status", "status")}</p><p>{formatMoney(value("totalAmount", "total_amount"))}</p><div className="card-list">{(value("deliverables", "deliverables") || []).map((item) => <article className="card" key={item.id}><strong>{item.title}</strong><p>{item.notes || "No notes"}</p></article>)}</div><div className="button-row">{value("status", "status") === "Offered" && <button className="button button--accent" onClick={() => openModal("contract-offer", { contract })}>Accept contract</button>}{value("status", "status") === "Submitted" && <button className="button button--accent" onClick={() => openModal("approve-contract", { onApprove: () => status.mutate({ contractId: id, status: "Completed" }) })}>Approve work</button>}{value("status", "status") === "Active" && <button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => openModal("client-dispute", { contractId: id })}>Raise dispute</button>}{value("status", "status") === "Active" && <form className="form-card" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); revision.mutate({ contractId: id, reason }); }}><h2>Request a revision</h2><input required value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} placeholder="Explain what needs changing" /><button className="button button--secondary" disabled={revision.isPending}>Request revision</button></form>}</div></section>;
}

export const contractStates = {
  offered: { label: "Offered", clientAction: "Accept contract", freelancerAction: null },
  active: { label: "Active", clientAction: "Request revision", freelancerAction: "Submit deliverable" },
  submitted: { label: "Submitted", clientAction: "Approve work", freelancerAction: null },
  completed: { label: "Completed", clientAction: null, freelancerAction: null },
  disputed: { label: "Disputed", clientAction: "Raise dispute", freelancerAction: "Raise dispute" },
  cancelled: { label: "Cancelled", clientAction: null, freelancerAction: null },
};

export function getContractState(status) {
  return contractStates[String(status || "").toLowerCase()] || {
    label: status || "Unknown",
    clientAction: null,
    freelancerAction: null,
  };
}

CONTENT:
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { acceptContract, createReview, requestContractRevision, updateContractStatus, verifyPayment } from "./contractApi.js";

const refreshContracts = (client) => {
  client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["contracts"] });
  client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["contract"] });
};

export const useVerifyPayment = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: verifyPayment, onSuccess: () => refreshContracts(client) });
};
export const useRequestRevision = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: requestContractRevision, onSuccess: () => refreshContracts(client) });
};
export const useUpdateContractStatus = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: updateContractStatus, onSuccess: () => refreshContracts(client) });
};
export const useAcceptContract = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: acceptContract, onSuccess: () => refreshContracts(client) });
};
export const useCreateReview = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: createReview, onSuccess: () => refreshContracts(client) });
};

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\contract\paymentForm.js
CONTENT:
export const paymentFormSchema = {
  paypalOrderId: (value) => typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0,
  paypalAmountUsd: (value) => Number.isFinite(Number(value)) && Number(value) > 0,
};

export function validatePaymentForm(values) {
  return {
    valid: Object.entries(paymentFormSchema).every(([key, validate]) => validate(values[key])),
    errors: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(paymentFormSchema).filter(([key, validate]) => !validate(values[key])).map(([key]) => [key, "Invalid payment value."])),
  };
}

CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { useVerifyPayment } from "../contractMutations.js";
import { validatePaymentForm } from "../paymentForm.js";

export default function ContractOfferModal({ onClose, contract }) {
  const [orderId, setOrderId] = useState("");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(contract.totalAmount ?? contract.total_amount ?? "");
  const verify = useVerifyPayment();
  const submit = (event) => { event.preventDefault(); const values = { paypalOrderId: orderId, paypalAmountUsd: Number(amount) }; if (!validatePaymentForm(values).valid) return; verify.mutate({ contractId: contract.id, ...values }, { onSuccess: onClose }); };
  return <div className="modal-backdrop" role="presentation"><form className="modal-card form-card" onSubmit={submit}><h2>Fund contract with PayPal</h2><label>PayPal order ID<input required value={orderId} onChange={(event) => setOrderId(event.target.value)} /></label><label>USD amount<input required type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" value={amount} onChange={(event) => setAmount(event.target.value)} /></label><div className="button-row"><button type="button" className="button button--quiet" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button><button className="button button--accent" disabled={verify.isPending}>{verify.isPending ? "Verifying..." : "Verify payment"}</button></div></form></div>;
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { getContractState } from "../contractDisplay.js";

describe("contract state display", () => {
  it.each([
    ["Offered", "Accept contract", null],
    ["Active", "Request revision", "Submit deliverable"],
    ["Submitted", "Approve work", null],
    ["Completed", null, null],
  ])("maps %s to the correct client/freelancer actions", (status, clientAction, freelancerAction) => {
    const state = getContractState(status);
    expect(state.label).toBe(status);
    expect(state.clientAction).toBe(clientAction);
    expect(state.freelancerAction).toBe(freelancerAction);
  });

  it("falls back safely for unknown statuses", () => {
    expect(getContractState("future-state")).toEqual({ label: "future-state", clientAction: null, freelancerAction: null });
  });
});

CONTENT:
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createInvitation, fetchEligibleJobs, getFreelancerDetails, getFreelancerList } from "./findTalentApi.js";

export const useFreelancerList = (params = {}) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["freelancers", params], queryFn: () => getFreelancerList(params) });
export const useFreelancerDetails = (id) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["freelancer", id], queryFn: () => getFreelancerDetails(id), enabled: Boolean(id) });
export const useEligibleProjects = (enabled = true) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["eligible-projects"], queryFn: fetchEligibleJobs, enabled });
export const useSendInvitation = (options = {}) => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: createInvitation, ...options, onSuccess: (...args) => { client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["invitations"] }); options.onSuccess?.(...args); } });
};

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import InvitationModal from "./InvitationModal.jsx";

export default function FreelancerCard({ freelancer }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const profile = freelancer || {};
  const name = profile.fullName || profile.full_name || profile.name || "Freelancer";
  const headline = profile.tagline || profile.title || "Independent contractor";
  const rating = Number(profile.rating ?? profile.averageRating ?? 0);
  const skills = profile.skills || profile.topSkills || [];

  return (
    <>
      <article className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{name}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{headline}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700">★ {rating.toFixed(1)}</div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {skills.slice(0, 4).map((skill, index) => (
            <span key={`${skill?.name ?? skill ?? index}`} className="rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-700">
              {skill?.name ?? skill}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <Link to={`/find-talents/${profile.id ?? profile.Id}`} className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700">View profile</Link>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700">Invite</button>
        </div>
      </article>

      <InvitationModal freelancer={profile} isOpen={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />
    </>
  );

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\findtalent\components\InvitationModal.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useEligibleProjects, useSendInvitation } from "../findTalentQueries.js";

export default function InvitationModal({ freelancer, isOpen, onClose }) {
  const { register, handleSubmit, reset } = useForm({ defaultValues: { projectId: "" } });
  const { data: eligibleProjectsData } = useEligibleProjects(Boolean(isOpen && freelancer));
  const eligibleProjects = eligibleProjectsData?.results || eligibleProjectsData || [];
  const mutation = useSendInvitation();

  if (!isOpen || !freelancer) return null;

  const onSubmit = (values) => {
    if (!values.projectId) {
      toast.error("Select a project before sending an invitation.");
      return;
    }

    mutation.mutate(
      {
        freelancerId: freelancer.id ?? freelancer.Id,
        projectId: Number(values.projectId),
      },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.success("Invitation sent.");
          reset();
          onClose();
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          const message = error?.response?.data?.detail || error?.response?.data?.message || "Unable to send invitation.";
          toast.error(message);
        },
      }
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">Invite freelancer</p>
            <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{freelancer.fullName || freelancer.full_name || freelancer.name}</h3>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="rounded-full border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sm text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100">Close</button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="mt-6 space-y-5">
          <div>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Choose a project</label>
            <select {...register("projectId")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100">
              <option value="">Select a project</option>
              {(eligibleProjects || []).map((project) => (
                <option key={project.id ?? project.Id} value={project.id ?? project.Id}>
                  {project.title ?? project.Title}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" disabled={mutation.isPending} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60">
              {mutation.isPending ? "Sending..." : "Send invitation"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

CONTENT:
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Star } from "lucide-react";
import { useFreelancerReviews } from "./freelancerProfileQueries.js";

export default function AllFreelancerReviews() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { data: reviews = [], isLoading, isError, error } = useFreelancerReviews(id);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-sm text-slate-500">Loading reviews...</div>;
  if (isError) return <div className="p-8 text-sm text-red-500">{error?.response?.data?.detail || "Unable to load reviews."}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl space-y-6 px-4 py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Reviews</h1>
        <span className="text-sm text-slate-500">{reviews.length} total</span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        {(reviews.length > 0 ? reviews : []).map((review) => (
          <div key={review.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{review.reviewer_name || "Client"}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{review.project_title || "Project review"}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                {[...Array(5)].map((_, index) => (
                  <Star key={`${review.id}-${index}`} className={`h-4 w-4 ${index < (review.rating ?? 0) ? "fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400" : "text-slate-200"}`} />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm leading-7 text-slate-600">{review.comment || "This client did not leave a written review."}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

---END FILE---

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\freelancerprofile\FreelancerProfile.jsx
CONTENT:
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight, Briefcase, MapPin, Star } from "lucide-react";
import { useClientFreelancerProfile } from "./freelancerProfileQueries.js";

export default function FreelancerProfile() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { data: profile, isLoading, isError, error } = useClientFreelancerProfile(id);

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-sm text-slate-500">Loading freelancer profile...</div>;
  }

  if (isError || !profile) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-sm text-red-500">{error?.response?.data?.detail || "Unable to load freelancer profile."}</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-6 px-4 py-10">
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-xl font-bold text-white">
              {profile.full_name?.[0] ?? "F"}
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{profile.full_name}</h1>
              <p className="text-sm text-blue-600">{profile.tagline}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm text-slate-600">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-yellow-50 px-3 py-1 font-medium text-yellow-700">
              <Star className="h-4 w-4 fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400" /> {profile.average_rating ?? 0}
            </span>
            <span>{profile.review_count ?? 0} reviews</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.3fr_0.7fr]">
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">About</h2>
            <p className="text-sm leading-7 text-slate-600">{profile.bio || "This freelancer has not added a bio yet."}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Skills</h2>
              <Link to={`/find-talents/${id}/reviews`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700">
                View reviews <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Link>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {(profile.skills ?? []).map((skill, index) => (
                <span key={`${skill}-${index}`} className="rounded-full border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700">
                  {skill}
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Packages</h2>
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {profile.packages?.starter && (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Starter</p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">${profile.packages.starter.price ?? 0}</p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">{profile.packages.starter.delivery_days ?? 0} day delivery</p>
                  <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    {(Array.isArray(profile.packages.starter.description) ? profile.packages.starter.description : (profile.packages.starter.description || "").split("\n")).filter(Boolean).map((line, index) => (
                      <li key={`${line}-${index}`}>• {line}</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}

              {profile.packages?.pro && (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Pro</p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">${profile.packages.pro.price ?? 0}</p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">{profile.packages.pro.delivery_days ?? 0} day delivery</p>
                  <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    {(Array.isArray(profile.packages.pro.description) ? profile.packages.pro.description : (profile.packages.pro.description || "").split("\n")).filter(Boolean).map((line, index) => (
                      <li key={`${line}-${index}`}>• {line}</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Profile details</h2>
            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-sm text-slate-600">
              <li className="flex items-center gap-2"><Briefcase className="h-4 w-4 text-blue-600" /> {profile.category || "Category not set"}</li>
              <li className="flex items-center gap-2"><MapPin className="h-4 w-4 text-blue-600" /> {profile.location || "Location not shared"}</li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Portfolio</h2>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {(profile.portfolios ?? []).map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
                  {item.image_url ? <img src={item.image_url} alt={item.title} className="h-32 w-full object-cover" /> : null}

                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fetchFreelancerProfile, fetchFreelancerReviews } from "./freelancerProfileApi.js";

export const useClientFreelancerProfile = (id) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["freelancer", id], queryFn: () => fetchFreelancerProfile(id), enabled: Boolean(id) });
export const useFreelancerReviews = (id, params = {}) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["freelancer-reviews", id, params], queryFn: () => fetchFreelancerReviews(id, params), enabled: Boolean(id) });

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\homepage\components\ClientHeader.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import ProfileModal from "./ProfileModal.jsx";

export default function ClientHeader() {
  const user = useSelector((state) => state.auth.user);
  const location = useLocation();
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [profileOpen, setProfileOpen] = useState(false);
  const firstName = (user?.fullName ?? user?.FullName ?? "Client").split(" ")[0];
  const links = [["Dashboard", "/home"], ["Find talent", "/find-talents"], ["How it works", "/how-it-works"], ["My projects", "/my-projects"]];
  const isActive = (href) => location.pathname === href || location.pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);

  return (
    <header className="client-home-header">
      <Link className="public-header__brand" to="/" aria-label="WebOfWork home">
        <span className="public-header__mark">W</span>
        <span>WebOfWork</span>
      </Link>
      <button type="button" className="client-home-header__menu" aria-expanded={menuOpen} onClick={() => setMenuOpen((open) => !open)}>
        {menuOpen ? "Close" : "Menu"}
      </button>
      <nav className={menuOpen ? "client-home-header__nav client-home-header__nav--open" : "client-home-header__nav"} aria-label="Client navigation">
        {links.map(([label, href]) => <Link className={isActive(href) ? "client-home-header__link client-home-header__link--active" : "client-home-header__link"} key={href} to={href} onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}>{label}</Link>)}
        <Link className="button button--accent" to="/create-project" onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}>Post a project</Link>
        <button type="button" className="client-home-header__profile" aria-expanded={profileOpen} onClick={() => setProfileOpen((open) => !open)}>
          <span>{firstName}</span>
          <strong>{firstName.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}</strong>
        </button>
      </nav>
      {profileOpen && <ProfileModal onClose={() => setProfileOpen(false)} />}
    </header>
  );
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

export default function HomeFreelancerCard({ freelancer }) {
  if (!freelancer) return null;

  const name = freelancer.fullName ?? freelancer.FullName ?? freelancer.name ?? "Independent professional";
  const skills = (freelancer.skills ?? freelancer.Skills ?? []).slice(0, 3);
  const rating = Number(freelancer.averageRating ?? freelancer.AverageRating ?? 0);

  return (
    <article className="freelancer-card">
      <div className="freelancer-card__identity">
        <span className="freelancer-card__avatar" aria-hidden="true">{name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}</span>
        <div><h3>{name}</h3><p>{freelancer.tagline ?? freelancer.Tagline ?? "Ready for the next thoughtful project."}</p></div>
      </div>
      <p className="freelancer-card__bio">{freelancer.bio ?? freelancer.Bio ?? "A capable independent professional for focused work."}</p>
      <div className="freelancer-card__meta">
        <span>{rating > 0 ? `${rating.toFixed(1)} rating` : "New profile"}</span>
        <span>{skills.length ? skills.map((skill) => typeof skill === "string" ? skill : skill.name ?? skill.Name).join(" / ") : "Open to projects"}</span>
      </div>
      <Link className="text-link" to={`/find-talents/${freelancer.id ?? freelancer.Id}`}>View profile</Link>
    </article>
  );
}

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\homepage\components\ProfileModal.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { useDispatch, useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { logoutThunk } from "../../account/auth/authThunks.js";
import { useSwitchRole } from "../homePageMutation.js";

export default function ProfileModal({ onClose }) {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const user = useSelector((state) => state.auth.user);
  const { mutateAsync, isPending } = useSwitchRole();
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await dispatch(logoutThunk());
    onClose();
    navigate("/");
  };

  const handleSwitchRole = async () => {
    setError("");
    try {
      await mutateAsync({ role: "freelancer" });
      onClose();
      navigate("/freelancer");
    } catch {
      setError("We could not switch roles right now.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <aside className="client-profile-menu" aria-label="Profile menu">
      <strong>{user?.fullName ?? user?.FullName ?? "Your account"}</strong>
      <nav>
        <Link to="/profile" onClick={onClose}>Profile</Link>
        <Link to="/contracts" onClick={onClose}>Contracts</Link>
        <Link to="/profile/settings" onClick={onClose}>Settings</Link>
        <Link to="/support" onClick={onClose}>Support</Link>
      </nav>
      {error && <p className="client-profile-menu__error">{error}</p>}
      <button type="button" disabled={isPending} onClick={handleSwitchRole}>{isPending ? "Switching..." : "Switch to freelancer"}</button>
      <button type="button" className="client-profile-menu__logout" onClick={handleLogout}>Log out</button>
    </aside>
  );
}
---END FILE---

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\landingPage\Landingpage.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import Header from "./components/Header.jsx";
import HeroSection from "./components/HeroSection.jsx";
import StepsSection from "./components/StepsSection.jsx";
import CategoriesSection from "./components/CategoriesSection.jsx";
import Features from "./components/Features.jsx";
import Testimonials from "./components/Testimonials.jsx";
import CTASection from "./components/CTASection.jsx";
import Footer from "./components/Footer.jsx";

export default function Landingpage() {
  const user = useSelector((state) => state.auth.user);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;

    const role = String(user.role ?? user.Role ?? "").toLowerCase();
    const destination = role === "admin" || role === "3"
      ? "/admin"
      : role === "freelancer" || role === "2"
        ? "/freelancer"
        : "/home";

    navigate(destination, { replace: true });
  }, [navigate, user]);

  if (user) return null;
  return <><Header /><main className="public-page"><HeroSection /><StepsSection /><CategoriesSection /><Features /><Testimonials /><CTASection /></main><Footer /></>;
}

import axiosInstance from "../../../api/axiosInstance.js";

export const sendPhoneOtp = async ({ phone }) => (await axiosInstance.post("/freelancers/phone/send-otp", { phone })).data;
export const verifyPhoneOtp = async ({ phone, otp }) => (await axiosInstance.post("/freelancers/phone/verify-otp", { phone, otp })).data;
export const fetchFreelancerProfile = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/profile/freelancer/me")).data;
export const submitFreelancerOnboarding = async (payload) => (await axiosInstance.post("/freelancers/onboard", payload)).data;
export const createPortfolioItem = async (payload) => (await axiosInstance.post("/profile/freelancer/portfolio", payload)).data;
export const updatePaymentSettings = async ({ paypalEmail }) => (await axiosInstance.put("/freelancers/payment-settings", JSON.stringify(paypalEmail), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } })).data;

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\onboarding\OnBoardingContext.jsx
CONTENT:
import { createContext, useContext, useState } from "react";

const OnboardingContext = createContext(null);

export function OnboardingProvider({ children, initialValue = {} }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState(initialValue);
  const updateValues = (next) => setValues((current) => ({ ...current, ...next }));
  return <OnboardingContext.Provider value={{ values, updateValues, setValues }}>{children}</OnboardingContext.Provider>;
}

export const useOnboarding = () => {
  const context = useContext(OnboardingContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error("useOnboarding must be used inside OnboardingProvider");
  return context;
};

export const buildOnboardingPayload = (steps) => ({
  tagline: steps.tagline ?? "",
  bio: steps.bio ?? "",
  phone: steps.phone ?? "",
  experienceLevel: steps.experienceLevel ?? "",
  primaryCategory: steps.primaryCategory ?? steps.categoryId ?? null,
  skills: (steps.skills ?? []).map((skill) => typeof skill === "number" ? { skillId: skill, customName: "" } : {
    skillId: skill.skillId ?? null,
    customName: skill.customName ?? "",
  }),
  packages: (steps.packages ?? []).map((item) => ({
    packageType: item.packageType ?? item.title ?? "",
    price: Number(item.price ?? 0),
    deliveryDays: Number(item.deliveryDays ?? 0),
    description: item.description ?? "",
  })),
});

export const mergeOnboardingStep = (current, step) => ({ ...current, ...step });

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\onboarding\OnboardingManager.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useOnboarding } from "./OnBoardingContext.jsx";
import { buildOnboardingPayload } from "./onBoardingHelper.js";
import { useCreatePortfolioItem, useSubmitFreelancerOnboarding } from "./onBoardingMutations.js";
import StepOne from "./step1/StepOne.jsx";
import StepTwo from "./step2/StepTwo.jsx";
import StepThree from "./step3/StepThree.jsx";
import StepFour from "./step4/StepFour.jsx";
import StepFive from "./step5/StepFive.jsx";
import StepSix from "./step6/StepSix.jsx";

const steps = [StepOne, StepTwo, StepThree, StepFour, StepFive, StepSix];

export default function OnboardingManager() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { values, setValues } = useOnboarding();
  const currentStep = Number(values?.currentStep ?? 1);
  const { mutateAsync: submitOnboarding, isPending: isSubmitting } = useSubmitFreelancerOnboarding();
  const { mutateAsync: createPortfolioItem } = useCreatePortfolioItem();

  const updateStep = (stepPayload) => {
    setValues((current) => ({
      ...current,
      ...stepPayload,
      currentStep: currentStep + 1,
    }));
  };

  const goBack = () => {
    setValues((current) => ({
      ...current,
      currentStep: Math.max(1, currentStep - 1),
    }));
  };

  const handleFinalSubmit = async (stepPayload) => {
    try {
      const payload = buildOnboardingPayload({ ...values, ...stepPayload });
      await submitOnboarding(payload);

      const portfolioItems = Array.isArray(values.portfolio) ? values.portfolio : [];
      if (portfolioItems.length > 0) {
        await Promise.all(
          portfolioItems.map((item) =>
            createPortfolioItem({
              title: item.title,
              description: item.description,
              imageUrl: item.imageUrl,
            }),
          ),
        );
      }

      toast.success("Freelancer onboarding complete.");
      navigate("/freelancer/profile");
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error?.response?.data?.detail || "Unable to complete onboarding.");
    }
  };

  const CurrentStep = steps[currentStep - 1] ?? StepOne;

  return (
    <CurrentStep
      values={values}
      onNext={updateStep}
      onBack={goBack}
      onSubmitFinal={handleFinalSubmit}
      isSubmitting={isSubmitting}
    />
  );
}

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { sendPhoneOtp, submitFreelancerOnboarding, verifyPhoneOtp } from "./OnboardingAPI.js";
import { createPortfolioItem, updatePaymentSettings } from "./OnboardingAPI.js";

export const useSendPhoneOtp = () => useMutation({ mutationFn: sendPhoneOtp });
export const useVerifyPhoneOtp = () => useMutation({ mutationFn: verifyPhoneOtp });
export const useSubmitFreelancerOnboarding = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: submitFreelancerOnboarding, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["freelancer-profile"] }) });
};
export const useCreatePortfolioItem = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: createPortfolioItem, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["freelancer-profile"] }) });
};
export const useUpdateOnboardingPaymentSettings = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: updatePaymentSettings, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["payment-settings"] }) });
};
