CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, ShieldCheck, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useSendPhoneOtp, useVerifyPhoneOtp } from "../onBoardingMutations.js";

export default function PhoneOtpModal({ isOpen, phone, onClose, onVerified }) {
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState("");
  const { mutateAsync: sendOtp, isPending: isSending } = useSendPhoneOtp();
  const { mutateAsync: verifyOtp, isPending: isVerifying } = useVerifyPhoneOtp();

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!phone?.trim()) {
      toast.error("Enter a phone number first.");
      return;
    }

    await sendOtp({ phone });
    toast.success("Verification code sent.");
  };

  const handleVerify = async () => {
    if (!otp.trim()) {
      toast.error("Enter the OTP code.");
      return;
    }

    await verifyOtp({ phone, otp });
    toast.success("Phone verified.");
    onVerified?.();
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/40 p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 p-2 text-blue-600">
              <Smartphone className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Verify phone</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">One-time passcode</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="text-sm text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700">Close</button>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3 text-sm text-slate-700">
          Sending OTP to <span className="font-semibold">{phone}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">OTP code</label>
            <input
              value={otp}
              onChange={(event) => setOtp(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="123456"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSend}
              disabled={isSending}
              className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isSending ? <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />Sending</span> : "Send code"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleVerify}
              disabled={isVerifying}
              className="flex-1 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {isVerifying ? <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />Verifying</span> : <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" />Verify</span>}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { CheckCircle2, Info, Phone } from "lucide-react";
import PhoneOtpModal from "./PhoneOtpModal.jsx";
import { stepOneSchema } from "./stepOneSchema.js";

export default function StepOne({ values, onNext, onBack }) {
  const [isPhoneModalOpen, setIsPhoneModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    getValues,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(stepOneSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      tagline: values.tagline ?? "",
      bio: values.bio ?? "",
      phone: values.phone ?? "",
      experienceLevel: values.experienceLevel ?? "beginner",
    },
  });

  const phone = getValues("phone") || "";

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    onNext({ ...data });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-6">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">Step 1</p>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Tell clients who you are</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Add your headline, location of expertise, and contact details so clients can trust your work.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Professional tagline</label>
            <input
              {...register("tagline")}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              placeholder="I design clean mobile-first product experiences for founders."
            />
            {errors.tagline && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-500">{errors.tagline.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Bio</label>
            <textarea
              {...register("bio")}
              rows={5}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              placeholder="I help founders launch polished products, landing pages, and user-friendly workflows with strong attention to detail."
            />
            {errors.bio && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-500">{errors.bio.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Phone number</label>
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <Phone className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                <input
                  {...register("phone")}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                  placeholder="+94 77 123 4567"
                />
              </div>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsPhoneModalOpen(true)} className="rounded-xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-100">
                Verify
              </button>
            </div>
            {errors.phone && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-500">{errors.phone.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Experience level</label>
            <select
              {...register("experienceLevel")}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            >
              <option value="beginner">Beginner</option>
              <option value="intermediate">Intermediate</option>
              <option value="expert">Expert</option>
            </select>
            {errors.experienceLevel && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-500">{errors.experienceLevel.message}</p>}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-700">
            <Info className="h-4 w-4" />
            Keep your profile clear and client-focused.
          </div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700">
            <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            {phone ? "Phone ready" : "Phone not verified yet"}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
          <button type="button" onClick={onBack} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100">

      <PhoneOtpModal isOpen={isPhoneModalOpen} phone={phone} onClose={() => setIsPhoneModalOpen(false)} onVerified={() => {}} />
    </>
  );

export const stepOneSchema = z.object({

	bio: z.string().min(50).max(500),
	phone: z.string().min(10),

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\onboarding\step2\StepTwo.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Check, Search, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { useCategories, useSkills } from "../../projectManagement/projectQueries.js";

export default function StepTwo({ values, onNext, onBack }) {
  const { data: categoriesData } = useCategories();
  const categories = useMemo(() => {
    if (Array.isArray(categoriesData?.results)) return categoriesData.results;
    if (Array.isArray(categoriesData)) return categoriesData;
    return [];
  }, [categoriesData]);

  const [selectedCategoryId, setSelectedCategoryId] = useState(values.primaryCategory ?? values.categoryId ?? categories[0]?.id ?? "");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [selectedSkills, setSelectedSkills] = useState(Array.isArray(values.skills) ? values.skills : []);

  const { data: skillsData } = useSkills(selectedCategoryId ? { categoryId: selectedCategoryId } : {});
  const skills = useMemo(() => {
    if (Array.isArray(skillsData?.results)) return skillsData.results;
    if (Array.isArray(skillsData)) return skillsData;
    return [];
  }, [skillsData]);

  const filteredSkills = useMemo(
    () => skills.filter((skill) => (skill.name ?? skill.customName ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())),
    [skills, search],
  );

  const toggleSkill = (skill) => {
    const skillId = skill.id ?? skill.skillId ?? skill.customName ?? null;
    const skillEntry = { skillId, customName: skill.name ?? skill.customName ?? "" };
    setSelectedSkills((current) => {
      const exists = current.some((entry) => Number(entry.skillId ?? entry.id ?? 0) === Number(skillId ?? 0));
      return exists ? current.filter((entry) => Number(entry.skillId ?? entry.id ?? 0) !== Number(skillId ?? 0)) : [...current, skillEntry];
    });
  };

  const onSubmit = () => {
    if (!selectedCategoryId || selectedSkills.length === 0) return;
    onNext({
      primaryCategory: Number(selectedCategoryId),
      categoryId: Number(selectedCategoryId),
      skills: selectedSkills,
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">Step 2</p>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Choose your services</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Select a category and the skills you want clients to discover.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <div>
          <h3 className="mb-3 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-slate-500">Category</h3>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {categories.map((category) => (
              <button
                key={category.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategoryId(category.id)}
                className={`rounded-full border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium ${
                  Number(selectedCategoryId) === Number(category.id)
                    ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-600 text-white"
                    : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100"
                }`}
              >
                {category.name}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-slate-500">Skills</h3>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{selectedSkills.length} selected</span>
          </div>

          <div className="relative mb-4">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search skills..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {filteredSkills.length > 0 ? (
              filteredSkills.map((skill) => {
                const skillId = skill.id ?? skill.skillId ?? skill.customName ?? "";
                const isSelected = selectedSkills.some((entry) => Number(entry.skillId ?? entry.id ?? 0) === Number(skillId ?? 0));
                return (
                  <button
                    key={skillId}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleSkill(skill)}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-3 py-2 text-sm ${
                      isSelected ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700" : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isSelected ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />}
                    {skill.name ?? skill.customName}
                  </button>
                );
              })
            ) : (
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">No skills available for the selected category yet.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
        <button type="button" onClick={onBack} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100">
          Back
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={!selectedCategoryId || selectedSkills.length === 0}
          onClick={onSubmit}
          className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300"
        >
          Continue
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";

const emptyPackage = {
  packageType: "Starter",
  price: 149,
  deliveryDays: 3,
  description: "",
};

export default function StepThree({ values, onNext, onBack }) {
  const [packages, setPackages] = useState(
    Array.isArray(values.packages) && values.packages.length > 0
      ? values.packages
      : [
          { ...emptyPackage },
          { ...emptyPackage, packageType: "Pro", price: 349, deliveryDays: 7 },
        ],
  );

  const packageLabels = useMemo(() => ["Starter", "Pro"], []);

  const updatePackage = (index, field, value) => {
    setPackages((current) => current.map((item, itemIndex) => (itemIndex === index ? { ...item, [field]: value } : item)));
  };

  const addPackage = () => {
    setPackages((current) => [...current, { ...emptyPackage, packageType: `Custom ${current.length + 1}` }]);
  };

  const removePackage = (index) => {
    setPackages((current) => (current.length > 1 ? current.filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index) : current));
  };

  const onSubmit = () => {
    if (packages.length === 0) return;
    onNext({ packages });
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">Step 3</p>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Set your service packages</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Create clear deliverables and transparent pricing for clients.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        {packages.map((pkg, index) => (
          <div key={`${pkg.packageType}-${index}`} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <select
                value={pkg.packageType}
                onChange={(event) => updatePackage(index, "packageType", event.target.value)}
                className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              >
                {packageLabels.map((label) => (
                  <option key={label} value={label}>{label}</option>
                ))}
              </select>

              {packages.length > 1 && (
                <button type="button" onClick={() => removePackage(index)} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100">
                  <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /> Remove
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Price</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  value={pkg.price}
                  onChange={(event) => updatePackage(index, "price", Number(event.target.value))}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Delivery days</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  value={pkg.deliveryDays}
                  onChange={(event) => updatePackage(index, "deliveryDays", Number(event.target.value))}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Title</label>
                <input
                  value={pkg.packageType}
                  onChange={(event) => updatePackage(index, "packageType", event.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4">
              <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Package description</label>
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={pkg.description}
                onChange={(event) => updatePackage(index, "description", event.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                placeholder="What is included in this package?"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}

        <button type="button" onClick={addPackage} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> Add package
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
        <button type="button" onClick={onBack} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100">
          Back
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={onSubmit} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700">
          Continue
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

import { useState } from "react";
import { ImagePlus, X } from "lucide-react";

const emptyPortfolioItem = {
  title: "",
  description: "",
  imageUrl: "",
};

export default function StepFour({ values, onNext, onBack }) {
  const [portfolio, setPortfolio] = useState(Array.isArray(values.portfolio) && values.portfolio.length > 0 ? values.portfolio : [emptyPortfolioItem]);

  const updateItem = (index, field, value) => {
    setPortfolio((current) => current.map((item, itemIndex) => (itemIndex === index ? { ...item, [field]: value } : item)));
  };

  const addItem = () => setPortfolio((current) => [...current, { ...emptyPortfolioItem }]);

  const removeItem = (index) => setPortfolio((current) => current.filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index));

  const onSubmit = () => {
    const sanitized = portfolio.filter((item) => item.title || item.description || item.imageUrl);
    if (sanitized.length === 0) {
      onNext({ portfolio: [] });
      return;
    }
    onNext({ portfolio: sanitized });
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">Step 4</p>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Show your best work</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Add portfolio case studies or product samples that demonstrate your quality.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        {portfolio.map((item, index) => (
          <div key={`${item.title || "portfolio"}-${index}`} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">Project #{index + 1}</span>
              {portfolio.length > 1 && (
                <button type="button" onClick={() => removeItem(index)} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100">
                  <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Remove
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Project title</label>
                <input
                  value={item.title}
                  onChange={(event) => updateItem(index, "title", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Brand identity refresh"
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"

              </div>
            </div>

              <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Short description</label>

import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { CheckCircle2, ClipboardCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { stepFiveSchema } from "./stepFiveSchema.js";

export default function StepFive({ values, onNext, onBack }) {
  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(stepFiveSchema),
    defaultValues: { confirmed: false },
  });

  const packages = Array.isArray(values.packages) ? values.packages : [];
  const portfolio = Array.isArray(values.portfolio) ? values.portfolio : [];

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    if (data.confirmed) {
      onNext({ confirmed: true });
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-6">

                  <span key={`${skill.skillId || skill.id || "custom"}-${index}`} className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700">

          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-700">{portfolio.length} items ready to publish</p>

        <label className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 text-sm text-slate-700">

          <span>
            I confirm this profile summary is ready to publish and my contact details are accurate.

import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { CreditCard } from "lucide-react";
import { stepSixSchema } from "./stepSixSchema.js";

export default function StepSix({ values, onBack, onSubmitFinal, isSubmitting }) {
  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(stepSixSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      paypalEmail: values.paypalEmail ?? "",
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data) => {
    await onSubmitFinal({ ...data });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">Step 6</p>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Add payout details</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">This is where your earnings are sent after client approval.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 p-3 text-blue-700">
          <CreditCard className="h-5 w-5" />
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">PayPal email</span>
        </div>

        <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">PayPal account email</label>
        <input
          {...register("paypalEmail")}
          type="email"
          placeholder="you@example.com"
          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
        />
        {errors.paypalEmail && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-500">{errors.paypalEmail.message}</p>}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
        <button type="button" onClick={onBack} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100">
          Back
        </button>
        <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300">
          {isSubmitting ? "Submitting..." : "Finish onboarding"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import OnboardingManager from "../OnboardingManager.jsx";
import { stepOneSchema } from "../step1/stepOneSchema.js";
import { stepTwoSchema } from "../step2/stepTwoSchema.js";
import { stepThreeSchema } from "../step3/stepThreeSchema.js";
import { stepFourSchema } from "../step4/stepFourSchema.js";
import { stepFiveSchema } from "../step5/stepFiveSchema.js";
import { stepSixSchema } from "../step6/stepSixSchema.js";

describe("freelancer onboarding flow", () => {
  it("exports the onboarding manager component", () => {
    expect(OnboardingManager).toBeTypeOf("function");
  });

  it("validates each onboarding step schema", () => {
    expect(stepOneSchema.safeParse({
      tagline: "I build polished web products for small businesses.",
      bio: "I design and ship clean digital experiences for founders, agencies, and teams who need reliable execution.",
      phone: "+94771234567",
      experienceLevel: "intermediate",
    }).success).toBe(true);

    expect(stepTwoSchema.safeParse({
      categoryId: 5,
      skills: [{ skillId: 7, customName: "" }],
    }).success).toBe(true);

    expect(stepThreeSchema.safeParse({
      packages: [{
        packageType: "Starter",
        price: 149,
        deliveryDays: 5,
        description: "Landing page design and front-end polish.",
      }],
    }).success).toBe(true);

      portfolio: [{
        title: "Moodboard app",
        description: "Case study for a product launch landing page.",
        imageUrl: "https://example.com/portfolio.png",
      }],
    }).success).toBe(true);

    expect(stepFiveSchema.safeParse({ confirmed: true }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(stepSixSchema.safeParse({ paypalEmail: "freelancer@example.com" }).success).toBe(true);
  });
});

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\profile\AccountSettings.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { changePassword } from "./profileApi.js";
import ForgotPasswordModal from "../account/components/ForgotPasswordModal.jsx";
import DeleteAccountModal from "../account/components/DeleteAccountModal.jsx";

const initialPasswordValues = {
  currentPassword: "",
  newPassword: "",
  confirmPassword: "",
};

export default function AccountSettings() {
  const user = useSelector((state) => state.auth.user);
  const [passwordValues, setPasswordValues] = useState(initialPasswordValues);
  const [passwordError, setPasswordError] = useState("");
  const [passwordSuccess, setPasswordSuccess] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [showForgotModal, setShowForgotModal] = useState(false);
  const [showDeleteModal, setShowDeleteModal] = useState(false);

  const updatePassword = (event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;
    setPasswordValues((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
  };

  const handlePasswordSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPasswordError("");
    setPasswordSuccess("");

    if (!passwordValues.currentPassword || !passwordValues.newPassword) {
      setPasswordError("Current and new passwords are required.");
      return;
    }

    if (passwordValues.newPassword !== passwordValues.confirmPassword) {
      setPasswordError("The new passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    if (passwordValues.newPassword.length < 8) {
      setPasswordError("New password must be at least 8 characters long.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);

    try {
      await changePassword({
        currentPassword: passwordValues.currentPassword,
        newPassword: passwordValues.newPassword,
      });

      setPasswordSuccess("Password updated successfully.");
      setPasswordValues(initialPasswordValues);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setPasswordError(requestError.response?.data?.error ?? "Unable to update your password.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 20 }}>
      <section style={{ background: "#fff", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, boxShadow: "0 10px 35px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06)" }}>
        <h3 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Account security</h3>
        <p style={{ marginTop: 0, color: "#475569" }}>Signed in as {user?.email ?? "your account"}.</p>

        <form onSubmit={handlePasswordSubmit} noValidate>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 16 }}>
            <label className="auth-field">
              Current password
              <input name="currentPassword" type="password" value={passwordValues.currentPassword} onChange={updatePassword} autoComplete="current-password" />
            </label>
            <label className="auth-field">
              New password
              <input name="newPassword" type="password" value={passwordValues.newPassword} onChange={updatePassword} autoComplete="new-password" />
            </label>
            <label className="auth-field">
              Confirm new password
              <input name="confirmPassword" type="password" value={passwordValues.confirmPassword} onChange={updatePassword} autoComplete="new-password" />
            </label>
          </div>

          {passwordError && <div className="admin-form-error" role="alert">{passwordError}</div>}
          {passwordSuccess && <div className="admin-form-success" role="status">{passwordSuccess}</div>}

          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, flexWrap: "wrap", marginTop: 18 }}>
            <button type="submit" className="button button--accent" disabled={pending}>{pending ? "Updating..." : "Update password"}</button>
            <button type="button" className="button button--secondary" onClick={() => setShowForgotModal(true)}>Forgot password</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section style={{ background: "#fff", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, boxShadow: "0 10px 35px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06)" }}>
        <h3 style={{ marginTop: 0, color: "#7f1d1d" }}>Danger zone</h3>
        <p style={{ color: "#475569" }}>Delete this account permanently. A confirmation code will be requested before the removal proceeds.</p>
        <button type="button" className="button button--danger" onClick={() => setShowDeleteModal(true)}>Delete account</button>
      </section>

      <ForgotPasswordModal isOpen={showForgotModal} initialEmail={user?.email ?? ""} onClose={() => setShowForgotModal(false)} />
      <DeleteAccountModal isOpen={showDeleteModal} onClose={() => setShowDeleteModal(false)} />
    </div>
  );
}

import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Camera, MapPin, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { uploadToCloudinary } from "../../../utils/cloudinaryHelper.js";
import { useProfile } from "./profileQueries.js";
import { useUpdateProfile } from "./profileMutations.js";
import { profileSchema } from "./profileSchema.js";

export default function EditProfile() {
  const { user } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
  const { data: profile, isLoading } = useProfile();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
  const [selectedImagePreview, setSelectedImagePreview] = useState(null);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const previewUrl = selectedImagePreview ?? profile?.profile_photo ?? user?.profile_photo ?? null;

  const { mutateAsync: updateProfile } = useUpdateProfile();

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    setValue,
    formState: { errors, isDirty },
  } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(profileSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      fullName: profile?.full_name || "",
      companyName: profile?.company_name || "",
      location: profile?.location || "",
    },
  });

  const handleImageChange = (event) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setValue("profileImage", file, { shouldDirty: true });

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onloadend = () => setSelectedImagePreview(reader.result);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const onSubmit = async (data) => {
    try {
      setIsUploading(true);

      let profilePhotoUrl = user?.profile_photo || profile?.profile_photo || null;

      if (data.profileImage) {
        const uploadRes = await uploadToCloudinary(data.profileImage);
        profilePhotoUrl = uploadRes.secure_url;
      }

      await updateProfile({
        full_name: data.fullName,
        company_name: data.companyName,
        location: data.location,
        profile_photo: profilePhotoUrl,
      });

      toast.success("Profile updated");
      navigate("/profile");
    } finally {
      setIsUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) return <Loader2 className="animate-spin" />;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 py-8 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">Edit Profile</h1>

        <section className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-4">Profile Photo</h2>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
            <div className="relative group cursor-pointer" onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}>
              <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-2xl font-bold overflow-hidden border-4 border-white shadow-md">
                {previewUrl ? (
                  <img src={previewUrl} alt="Preview" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                ) : (
                  user?.full_name?.[0]?.toUpperCase() || "UN"
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                <Camera className="text-white w-6 h-6" />
              </div>

              <input ref={fileInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={handleImageChange} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-50">
                Change Photo
              </button>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">JPG, PNG or WEBP. Max 5MB.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wider">Basic Information</h2>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Full Name *</label>
              <input
                {...register("fullName")}
                className={`w-full p-2.5 border rounded-lg outline-none focus:ring-2 ${errors.fullName ? "border-red-500 focus:ring-red-200" : "border-gray-300 focus:ring-blue-500"}`}
              />
              {errors.fullName && <p className="text-red-500 text-xs mt-1">{errors.fullName.message}</p>}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Company Name <span className="text-gray-400">(Optional)</span></label>
              <input {...register("companyName")} className="w-full p-2.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500" />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Location <span className="text-gray-400">(Optional)</span></label>
              <div className="relative">
                <MapPin className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 w-4 h-4" />
                <input {...register("location")} className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 pt-4">
          <button type="submit" disabled={isUploading || !isDirty} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:bg-blue-300 flex items-center gap-2">
            {isUploading && <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />}
            Save Changes
          </button>

          <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate("/profile")} className="px-6 py-2.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-gray-50">
            Cancel
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\profile\Interests.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { X, Plus, Search } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useCategories } from "../projectManagement/projectQueries.js";
import { useProfile } from "./profileQueries.js";
import { useUpdateProfile } from "./profileMutations.js";

export default function Interests() {
  const { data: categoriesData } = useCategories();
  const { data: profile } = useProfile();
  const { mutate: updateProfile, isPending } = useUpdateProfile();

  const categories = useMemo(() => categoriesData?.results ?? [], [categoriesData]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState([]);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");

  const initialSelectedIds = useMemo(() => {
    if (!profile?.categories || categories.length === 0) return new Set();
    return new Set(
      categories
        .filter((cat) => profile.categories.includes(cat.name) || profile.categories.includes(cat.id))
        .map((cat) => cat.id),
    );
  }, [profile, categories]);

  const derivedSelected = useMemo(() => {
    const byId = new Map();
    categories.filter((category) => initialSelectedIds.has(category.id)).forEach((category) => byId.set(category.id, category));
    selected.forEach((category) => byId.set(category.id, category));
    return Array.from(byId.values());
  }, [categories, initialSelectedIds, selected]);

  const handleSave = () => {
    updateProfile(
      { categories: derivedSelected.map((category) => category.id) },
      {
        onSuccess: () => toast.success("Updated successfully"),
        onError: () => toast.error("Failed to update interests"),
      },
    );
  };

  const filteredCategories = categories.filter((category) => category.name?.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()));
  const popularCategories = categories.slice(0, 10);

  const addCategory = (category) => {
    if (!selected.find((item) => item.id === category.id)) {
      setSelected((current) => [...current, category]);
    }
    setSearchQuery("");
  };

  const removeCategory = (category) => {
    setSelected((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== category.id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-white py-8 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="max-w-[800px] mx-auto">
        <h1 className="text-[26px] font-bold text-[#111827] mb-8">Skills & Interests</h1>

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="bg-white border border-[#F3F4F6] rounded-xl shadow p-8">
            <div className="mb-6">
              <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-[#111827] mb-2">Current Interests</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#6B7280]">These skills appear on your profile and help match you with the right freelancers.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              {derivedSelected.length > 0 ? (
                derivedSelected.map((cat) => <InterestTag key={cat.id} label={cat.name} onRemove={() => removeCategory(cat)} />)
              ) : (
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">No skills selected yet.</p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white border border-[#F3F4F6] rounded-xl shadow p-8">
            <div className="mb-6">
              <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-[#111827] mb-2">Add New Interests</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#6B7280]">Search or choose from popular categories below.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="relative mb-8">
              <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-[#9CA3AF]" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(event) => setSearchQuery(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search categories..."
                className="w-full h-[50px] pl-12 pr-4 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-medium text-[#374151] mb-4">Popular Categories</h3>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                {(searchQuery ? filteredCategories : popularCategories)
                  .filter((cat) => !derivedSelected.find((item) => item.id === cat.id))
                  .map((cat) => (
                    <SkillButton key={cat.id} label={cat.name} onClick={() => addCategory(cat)} />
                  ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-8">
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6B7280]">{derivedSelected.length} skills selected</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSave}
            disabled={isPending}
            className="bg-[#3B82F6] text-white px-8 py-[15px] rounded-lg text-[15px] font-medium hover:bg-[#2563EB] transition disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            Save Interests
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function InterestTag({ label, onRemove }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#EFF6FF] border border-[#DBEAFE] rounded-full px-[17px] py-[10px] text-xs font-medium text-[#3B82F6]">
      {label}
      <button type="button" onClick={onRemove} className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-[#DBEAFE] flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-[#BFDBFE]">
        <X className="w-3 h-3 text-[#3B82F6]" />
      </button>
    </span>
  );
}

function SkillButton({ label, onClick }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-[17px] py-[10px] text-xs font-medium border transition bg-[#F9FAFB] border-[#E5E7EB] text-[#374151] hover:bg-[#F3F4F6]"
    >
      <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}

import axiosInstance from "../../../api/axiosInstance.js";

export const getMyProfile = async () => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.get("/profile/me");
  return response.data;
};

export const updateProfile = async (payload) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.patch("/profile/me", payload);
  return response.data;
};

export const changePassword = async (payload) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.post("/auth/change-password", payload);
  return response.data;
};

export const fetchClientSpendingSummary = async () => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.get("/payments/client/spending");
  return response.data;
};

export const fetchClientReviews = async (userId, page = 1) => {
  const response = await axiosInstance.get("/reviews/me/client", {
    params: userId ? { userId, page } : { page },
  });
  return response.data;
};

CONTENT:
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useDispatch, useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { setCredentials } from "../account/auth/authslice.js";
import { changePassword, updateProfile } from "./profileApi.js";

export const useUpdateProfile = () => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const currentUser = useSelector((state) => state.auth.user);

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: updateProfile,
    onSuccess: (updatedProfile) => {
      const user = { ...currentUser, ...updatedProfile };
      dispatch(setCredentials({ user }));
      queryClient.setQueryData(["profile", "me"], updatedProfile);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["profile", "me"] });
    },
  });
};

export const useChangePassword = () =>
  useMutation({ mutationFn: changePassword });

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Calendar, Building2, MapPin, Star, MessageSquare, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { useClientReviews, useProfile } from "./profileQueries.js";

export default function ProfileOverview() {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const { data: profile, isLoading: profileLoading, isError: profileError } = useProfile();
  const user = useSelector((state) => state.auth.user);
  const { data: reviewsData, isLoading: reviewsLoading, isFetching } = useClientReviews(user?.id, page);

  const reviews = reviewsData?.results ?? [];
  const hasNext = Boolean(reviewsData?.next);
  const hasPrev = Boolean(reviewsData?.previous);
  const totalCount = reviewsData?.count ?? 0;

  if (profileLoading) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-gray-500 animate-pulse font-medium">Loading profile...</div>;
  }

  if (profileError || !profile) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-red-500 font-medium">Failed to load profile</div>;
  }

  const { full_name, email, profile_photo, company_name, location, categories, joined_at, average_rating, review_count } = profile;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-[26px] lg:mt-10 font-black text-gray-900 mb-2">Profile Overview</h1>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
        <div className="p-6 md:p-8">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center md:items-start gap-6">
            <div className="relative">
              <img
                src={profile_photo || `https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=${encodeURIComponent(full_name || "Client")}&background=3B82F6&color=fff`}
                alt="Profile"
                className="w-24 h-24 md:w-32 md:h-32 rounded-3xl object-cover shadow-lg border-4 border-white"
              />
              <div className="absolute -bottom-2 -right-2 bg-green-500 w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-4 border-white" />
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 w-full text-center md:text-left">
              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-gray-900 mb-1">{full_name || "Client"}</h2>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center md:justify-start items-center gap-3">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">{email}</p>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">
                      <Star className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500 fill-amber-500" />
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-700">{Number(average_rating || 0).toFixed(1)}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-amber-600/60">({review_count ?? 0})</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <Link to="/profile/edit" className="inline-flex justify-center px-6 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-md">
                  Edit Profile
                </Link>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row flex-wrap justify-center md:justify-start items-center gap-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Calendar className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Joined {joined_at ? new Date(joined_at).toLocaleDateString() : "—"}</span>
                </div>
                {company_name && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Building2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span className="text-gray-600">{company_name}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                {location && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <MapPin className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span className="text-gray-600">{location}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6">
        <section className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm p-6 md:p-8">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-gray-900 uppercase tracking-widest mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="w-1 h-4 bg-blue-600 rounded-full" />
            Skills & Interests
          </h3>
          {Array.isArray(categories) && categories.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {categories.map((cat) => (
                <span key={cat.id ?? cat} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 bg-gray-50 text-gray-600 text-xs font-bold transition-all hover:bg-white hover:border-blue-200 hover:text-blue-600">
                  {cat.name ?? cat}
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 italic">No skills listed yet.</p>
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm p-6 md:p-8">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-8">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-gray-900 uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-1 h-4 bg-blue-600 rounded-full" />
              Reviews ({totalCount})
            </h3>
            {isFetching && <span className="text-[10px] bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 px-2 py-1 rounded-md font-black animate-pulse">SYNCING</span>}
          </div>

          {reviewsLoading ? (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="h-24 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
              <div className="h-24 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
            </div>
          ) : reviews.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <div className="space-y-6">
                {reviews.map((review) => (
                  <div key={review.id} className="p-5 bg-gray-50/50 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 transition-all hover:bg-white hover:shadow-md">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
                      <img
                        src={review.reviewer_photo || `https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=${encodeURIComponent(review.reviewer_name || "Reviewer")}&background=3B82F6&color=fff`}
                        className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl object-cover border-2 border-white shadow-sm"
                        alt=""
                      />
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-black text-gray-900">{review.reviewer_name}</h4>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <div className="flex">
                            {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
                              <Star key={i} className={`w-3 h-3 ${i < Number(review.rating || 0) ? "text-amber-400 fill-amber-400" : "text-gray-200"}`} />
                            ))}
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest">
                            {review.created_at ? new Date(review.created_at).toLocaleDateString() : "—"}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 italic leading-relaxed pl-2 border-l-2 border-gray-200">
                      “{review.comment}”
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {(hasPrev || hasNext) && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-gray-100">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setPage((old) => Math.max(old - 1, 1))}
                    disabled={!hasPrev}
                    className="p-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl disabled:opacity-20 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-all active:scale-90"
                  >
                    <ChevronLeft className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-600" />
                  </button>
                  <span className="text-xs font-black text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">{page}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setPage((old) => (hasNext ? old + 1 : old))}
                    disabled={!hasNext}
                    className="p-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl disabled:opacity-20 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-all active:scale-90"
                  >
                    <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-600" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          ) : (
            <div className="text-center py-12 bg-gray-50 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200">
              <MessageSquare className="w-10 h-10 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">No reviews found</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fetchClientReviews, fetchClientSpendingSummary, getMyProfile } from "./profileApi.js";

export const useProfile = () =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: ["profile", "me"],
    queryFn: getMyProfile,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  });

export const useClientSpendingSummary = () =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: ["client-spending-summary"],
    queryFn: fetchClientSpendingSummary,
  });

export const useClientReviews = (userId, page = 1) =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: ["client-reviews", userId, page],
    queryFn: () => fetchClientReviews(userId, page),
    enabled: userId !== undefined && userId !== null,
  });

import { DollarSign, Briefcase, Clock, TrendingUp } from "lucide-react";
import { formatMoney } from "../../../utils/formatMoney.js";
import { useClientSpendingSummary } from "./profileQueries.js";

export default function SpendingSummary() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useClientSpendingSummary();

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center">Loading Summary...</div>;
  if (isError) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-red-500">Error loading data</div>;

  const stats = [
    {
      label: "Total Spent",
      value: formatMoney(data?.total_spent ?? 0),
      icon: DollarSign,
      bgColor: "bg-[#EFF6FF]",
      iconColor: "text-black",
    },
    {
      label: "Projects Completed",
      value: data?.projects_completed ?? 0,
      icon: Briefcase,
      bgColor: "bg-[#F0FDF4]",
      iconColor: "text-[#16A34A]",
    },
    {
      label: "Ongoing Projects",
      value: data?.ongoing_projects ?? 0,
      icon: Clock,
      bgColor: "bg-[#FFF7ED]",
      iconColor: "text-[#EA580C]",
    },
    {
      label: "Avg. per Project",
      value: formatMoney(data?.avg_per_project ?? 0),
      icon: TrendingUp,
      bgColor: "bg-[#FAF5FF]",
      iconColor: "text-[#9333EA]",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F7F7F7] px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl">
        <h1 className="mb-8 text-[26px] font-bold leading-9 text-[#111827]">Spending Summary</h1>

        <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 lg:gap-[67px]">
          {stats.map((stat, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="rounded-xl border border-[#F3F4F6] bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
              <div className={`mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${stat.bgColor}`}>
                <stat.icon className={`h-6 w-6 ${stat.iconColor}`} strokeWidth={2} />
              </div>
              <div className="mb-1 text-xl font-bold leading-8 text-[#111827]">{stat.value}</div>
              <div className="text-xs leading-5 text-[#6B7280]">{stat.label}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#F3F4F6] bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="mb-6 text-[15px] font-semibold leading-7 text-[#111827]">Recent Projects</h2>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            {data?.recent_projects?.map((project) => (
              <div key={project.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[#F3F4F6] p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <h3 className="mb-1 text-sm font-medium leading-6 text-[#111827]">{project.project_title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[#6B7280]">with {project.freelancer_name}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold leading-6 text-[#111827]">{formatMoney(project.total_amount)}</span>
                  <span className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-medium leading-4 capitalize ${project.status === "completed" ? "bg-[#F0FDF4] text-[#15803D]" : "bg-[#EFF6FF] text-[#1D4ED8]"}`}>
                    {String(project.status ?? "pending").replace("_", " ")}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}

            {(!data?.recent_projects || data.recent_projects.length === 0) && (
              <div className="py-4 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">No recent projects found.</div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";
import { User, Edit3, Sparkles, Settings, TrendingUp, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useModal } from "../../../../hooks/modal/useModalStore.jsx";

const navigation = [
  { name: "Profile Overview", href: "/profile", icon: User },
  { name: "Edit Profile", href: "/profile/edit", icon: Edit3 },
  { name: "Skills & Interests", href: "/profile/interests", icon: Sparkles },
  { name: "Account Settings", href: "/profile/settings", icon: Settings },
  { name: "Spending Summary", href: "/profile/spending", icon: TrendingUp },
];

export default function ProfileSidebar({ onNavigate }) {
  const { openModal } = useModal();

  return (
    <div className="h-full md:mt-8 px-6 pt-6 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">Client Account</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Manage your profile and settings</p>
      </div>

      <nav className="flex-1 space-y-1">
        {navigation.map((item) => (
          <NavLink
            key={item.name}
            to={item.href}
            end={item.href === "/profile"}
            onClick={onNavigate}
            className={({ isActive }) => `flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${isActive ? "bg-blue-500 text-white shadow-sm" : "text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"}`}

              </>
            )}
          </NavLink>
        ))}

        <div className="pt-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              openModal("delete-account");
              if (onNavigate) onNavigate();

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\projectManagement\AddProject.jsx
CONTENT:
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useCategories, useSkills } from "./projectQueries.js";
import { useCreateProject, useStartAiWorkflow } from "./projectMutations.js";
import { projectCreateSchema } from "./projectSchemas.js";

const defaultValues = {
  title: "",
  description: "",
  requirements: "",
  expectedDeliverables: "",
  pricingType: 1,
  fixedPrice: "",
  minPrice: "",
  maxPrice: "",
  expectedDays: "",
  categoryId: "",
  skillIds: [],
  customSkillNames: [],
};

export default function AddProject() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const mutation = useCreateProject();
  const startAi = useStartAiWorkflow();
  const { data: categoryData } = useCategories();
  const categories = categoryData?.results || categoryData || [];
  const { register, handleSubmit, watch, setValue, formState: { errors }, reset } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(projectCreateSchema),
    defaultValues,
  });
  const pricingType = Number(watch("pricingType") || 1);
  const categoryId = watch("categoryId");
  const { data: skillData } = useSkills({ categoryId: categoryId ? Number(categoryId) : undefined });
  const skillOptions = skillData?.results || skillData || [];

  const toggleSkill = (skillId) => {
    const current = watch("skillIds") || [];
    const next = current.includes(skillId)
      ? current.filter((id) => id !== skillId)
      : [...current, skillId];
    setValue("skillIds", next, { shouldDirty: true, shouldValidate: true });
  };

  const onSubmit = (values) => {
    const payload = {
      ...values,
      pricingType: Number(values.pricingType),
      fixedPrice: values.pricingType === 1 ? Number(values.fixedPrice) : null,
      minPrice: values.pricingType === 2 ? Number(values.minPrice) : null,
      maxPrice: values.pricingType === 2 ? Number(values.maxPrice) : null,
      expectedDays: Number(values.expectedDays),
      categoryId: values.categoryId ? Number(values.categoryId) : null,
      skillIds: (values.skillIds || []).map(Number),
      customSkillNames: values.customSkillNames || [],
    };

    mutation.mutate(payload, {
      onSuccess: (createdProject) => {
        toast.success("Project created.");
        if (createdProject?.id) startAi.mutate({ objective: values.title, plan: values.description, projectId: Number(createdProject.id) });
        reset(defaultValues);
        navigate("/my-projects");
      },
      onError: (error) => {
        const message = error?.response?.data?.detail || error?.response?.data?.message || "Unable to create project.";
        toast.error(message);
      },
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-blue-600">Marketplace</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Create a project</h1>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Project title</label>
            <input {...register("title")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.title && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.title.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Description</label>
            <textarea {...register("description")} rows={4} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.description && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.description.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Requirements</label>
            <textarea {...register("requirements")} rows={3} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.requirements && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.requirements.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Expected deliverables</label>
            <textarea {...register("expectedDeliverables")} rows={3} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.expectedDeliverables && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.expectedDeliverables.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Pricing type</label>
            <select {...register("pricingType")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100">
              <option value={1}>Fixed price</option>
              <option value={2}>Range</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Expected days</label>
            <input type="number" min="1" {...register("expectedDays")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.expectedDays && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.expectedDays.message}</p>}
          </div>

          {pricingType === 1 ? (
            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Fixed price (USD)</label>
              <input type="number" min="1" step="0.01" {...register("fixedPrice")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
              {errors.fixedPrice && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.fixedPrice.message}</p>}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Minimum price (USD)</label>
                <input type="number" min="1" step="0.01" {...register("minPrice")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
                {errors.minPrice && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.minPrice.message}</p>}
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Maximum price (USD)</label>
                <input type="number" min="1" step="0.01" {...register("maxPrice")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
                {errors.maxPrice && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.maxPrice.message}</p>}
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Category</label>
            <select {...register("categoryId")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100">
              <option value="">Select a category</option>
              {(categories || []).map((category) => (
                <option key={category.id ?? category.Id} value={category.id ?? category.Id}>
                  {category.name ?? category.Name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Skills</label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3">
              {(skillOptions || []).map((skill) => {
                const skillId = skill.id ?? skill.Id;
                const selected = (watch("skillIds") || []).includes(skillId);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={skillId}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleSkill(skillId)}
                    className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition ${selected ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700" : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:border-slate-300"}`}
                  >
                    {skill.name ?? skill.Name}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => startAi.mutate({ objective: watch("title"), plan: watch("description") })} disabled={startAi.isPending} className="rounded-xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-blue-700 disabled:opacity-60" title="Create the project first; the project-bound AI workflow starts automatically after creation.">{startAi.isPending ? "Starting AI..." : "Find matches with AI"}</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate("/my-projects")} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
          <button type="submit" disabled={mutation.isPending} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60">
            {mutation.isPending ? "Saving..." : "Create project"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}
