CONTENT:
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useCategories, useProjectDetails, useSkills } from "./projectQueries.js";
import { useUpdateProject } from "./projectMutations.js";
import { projectCreateSchema } from "./projectSchemas.js";

export default function EditProject() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const mutation = useUpdateProject();
  const { data: projectData, isLoading } = useProjectDetails(id);
  const { data: categoryData } = useCategories();
  const categories = categoryData?.results || categoryData || [];
  const { register, handleSubmit, watch, setValue, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm({
    resolver: zodResolver(projectCreateSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      title: "",
      description: "",
      requirements: "",
      expectedDeliverables: "",
      pricingType: 1,
      fixedPrice: "",
      minPrice: "",
      maxPrice: "",
      expectedDays: "",
      categoryId: "",
      skillIds: [],
      customSkillNames: [],
    },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!projectData) return;
    reset({
      title: projectData.title || "",
      description: projectData.description || "",
      requirements: projectData.requirements || "",
      expectedDeliverables: projectData.expectedDeliverables || "",
      pricingType: projectData.pricingType === "Range" ? 2 : 1,
      fixedPrice: projectData.fixedPrice ?? "",
      minPrice: projectData.minPrice ?? "",
      maxPrice: projectData.maxPrice ?? "",
      expectedDays: projectData.expectedDays ?? "",
      categoryId: projectData.categoryId ?? "",
      skillIds: (projectData.skills || []).map((skill) => skill.skillId ?? skill.id).filter(Boolean),
      customSkillNames: [],
    });
  }, [projectData, reset]);

  const categoryId = watch("categoryId");
  const { data: skillData } = useSkills({ categoryId: categoryId ? Number(categoryId) : undefined });
  const skillOptions = skillData?.results || skillData || [];
  const pricingType = Number(watch("pricingType") || 1);

  const toggleSkill = (skillId) => {
    const current = watch("skillIds") || [];
    const next = current.includes(skillId) ? current.filter((id) => id !== skillId) : [...current, skillId];
    setValue("skillIds", next, { shouldDirty: true, shouldValidate: true });
  };

  const onSubmit = (values) => {
    const payload = {
      title: values.title,
      description: values.description,
      requirements: values.requirements,
      expectedDeliverables: values.expectedDeliverables,
      expectedDays: Number(values.expectedDays),
      categoryId: values.categoryId ? Number(values.categoryId) : null,
      skillIds: (values.skillIds || []).map(Number),
      customSkillNames: values.customSkillNames || [],
    };

    mutation.mutate(
      { id: Number(id), data: payload },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.success("Project updated.");
          navigate(`/my-projects/${id}`);
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          const message = error?.response?.data?.detail || error?.response?.data?.message || "Unable to update project.";
          toast.error(message);
        },
      }
    );
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading project…</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-blue-600">Marketplace</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Edit project</h1>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Project title</label>
            <input {...register("title")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.title && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.title.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Description</label>
            <textarea {...register("description")} rows={4} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.description && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.description.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Requirements</label>
            <textarea {...register("requirements")} rows={3} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.requirements && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.requirements.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Expected deliverables</label>
            <textarea {...register("expectedDeliverables")} rows={3} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.expectedDeliverables && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.expectedDeliverables.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Expected days</label>
            <input type="number" min="1" {...register("expectedDays")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
            {errors.expectedDays && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{errors.expectedDays.message}</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Category</label>
            <select {...register("categoryId")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100">
              <option value="">Select a category</option>
              {(categories || []).map((category) => (
                <option key={category.id ?? category.Id} value={category.id ?? category.Id}>
                  {category.name ?? category.Name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Skills</label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3">
              {(skillOptions || []).map((skill) => {
                const skillId = skill.id ?? skill.Id;
                const selected = (watch("skillIds") || []).includes(skillId);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={skillId}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleSkill(skillId)}
                    className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition ${selected ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700" : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:border-slate-300"}`}
                  >
                    {skill.name ?? skill.Name}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
              Pricing type stays as configured on the original project; edit the price, budget, or range via the original project creation flow if needed.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate(`/my-projects/${id}`)} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
          <button type="submit" disabled={mutation.isPending} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60">
            {mutation.isPending ? "Saving..." : "Save changes"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useMyProjects } from "./projectQueries.js";
import ProjectCard from "./components/ProjectCard.jsx";

export default function MyProjects() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useMyProjects();
  const projects = data?.results || data || [];

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading projects…</div>;
  }

  if (isError) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-600">Failed to load your projects.</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-blue-600">Marketplace</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">My projects</h1>
        </div>
        <Link to="/create-project" className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700">Create project</Link>
      </div>

      {projects.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-12 text-center">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-slate-800">No projects created yet</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Launch your first project to start receiving proposals.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
          {projects.map((project) => (
            <ProjectCard key={project.id ?? project.Id} project={project} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import axiosInstance from "../../../api/axiosInstance.js";

export const fetchCategories = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/categories")).data;
export const fetchSkills = async (params = {}) => (await axiosInstance.get("/categories/skills", { params })).data;
export const fetchAllSkills = fetchSkills;
export const fetchAllCategories = fetchCategories;
export const createProject = async (payload) => (await axiosInstance.post("/projects", payload)).data;
export const fetchMyProjects = async ({ queryKey }) => {
  const [, params = {}] = queryKey;
  return (await axiosInstance.get("/projects/my", { params })).data;
};
export const fetchProjectById = async (id) => (await axiosInstance.get(`/projects/${id}`)).data;
export const updateProject = async ({ id, data }) => (await axiosInstance.put(`/projects/${id}`, data)).data;
export const deleteProject = async ({ id }) => (await axiosInstance.delete(`/projects/${id}`)).data;
export const fetchRecommendedProjects = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/projects/recommended")).data;

import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useProjectDetails } from "./projectQueries.js";
import { useAiWorkflowStatus } from "../../aiworkflow/aiWorkflowQueries.js";
import { useDeleteProject } from "./projectMutations.js";
import DeleteProjectModal from "./components/DeleteProjectModal.jsx";
import { useState } from "react";

export default function ProjectDetails() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useProjectDetails(id);
  const { data: workflow } = useAiWorkflowStatus(data?.workflowRunId ?? data?.aiWorkflowRunId);
  const mutation = useDeleteProject();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading project…</div>;
  if (isError || !data) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-600">Project not found.</div>;

  const budget = data.fixedPrice ?? data.minPrice ?? data.maxPrice ?? 0;
  const skills = data.skills || [];

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-blue-600">Project</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{data.title}</h1>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <Link to={`/my-projects/${id}/edit`} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Edit</Link>
          <Link to={`/my-projects/${id}/proposals`} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700">View proposals</Link>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirming(true)} className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100">Archive</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[2fr,1fr]">
        <div className="space-y-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-500">Overview</h2>
            <p className="mt-3 text-slate-700">{data.description}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4"><p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Budget</p><p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">${Number(budget || 0).toLocaleString()}</p></div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4"><p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Delivery</p><p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{data.expectedDays || 0} days</p></div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4"><p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Status</p><p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{data.status || "Open"}</p></div>
           {workflow && <div className="rounded-xl bg-blue-50 p-4"><p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-600">AI workflow</p><p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-blue-900">{workflow.status}</p></div>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Requirements</h3>
            <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-line text-slate-700">{data.requirements || "No requirements specified."}</p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Deliverables</h3>
            <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-line text-slate-700">{data.expectedDeliverables || "No deliverables specified."}</p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Skills</h3>
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {skills.length > 0 ? skills.map((skill) => (
                <span key={skill.skillId ?? skill.id ?? skill.name ?? skill.Name} className="rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
                  {skill.name ?? skill.Name ?? skill.skillName}
                </span>
              )) : <span className="text-sm text-slate-500">No skills selected.</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <aside className="space-y-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-500">Quick actions</h2>
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate(`/my-projects/${id}/proposals`)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Review proposals</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate("/find-talents")} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Find talent</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>

      <DeleteProjectModal
        project={data}
        pending={mutation.isPending}
        onClose={() => setConfirming(false)}
        isOpen={confirming}
        onConfirm={() => {
          mutation.mutate({ id: Number(id) }, { onSuccess: () => { setConfirming(false); navigate("/my-projects"); } });
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

CONTENT:
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createProject, deleteProject, updateProject } from "./ProjectApi.js";
import { startAiWorkflow } from "../aiworkflow/aiWorkflowApi.js";

export const useCreateProject = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: createProject, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["projects"] }) });
};
export const useUpdateProject = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: updateProject, onSuccess: (_, variables) => { client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["projects"] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["project", variables.id] }); } });
};
export const useDeleteProject = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteProject, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["projects"] }) });
};
export const useStartAiWorkflow = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: startAiWorkflow, onSuccess: () => { client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["ai-workflow"] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["project"] }); } });
};

import { fetchAllCategories, fetchAllSkills, fetchCategories, fetchMyProjects, fetchProjectById } from "./ProjectApi.js";

export const useSkills = (params = {}) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["skills", params], queryFn: () => fetchAllSkills(params) });
export const useAllSkillls = () => useQuery({ queryKey: ["skills", "all"], queryFn: fetchAllSkills });
export const useAllSkills = useAllSkillls;
export const useAllCategories = () => useQuery({ queryKey: ["categories", "all"], queryFn: fetchAllCategories });
export const useMyProjects = (params = {}) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["projects", params], queryFn: fetchMyProjects });
export const useProjectDetails = (id) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["project", id], queryFn: () => fetchProjectById(id), enabled: Boolean(id) });

CONTENT:
import { z } from "zod";

export const projectCreateSchema = z
  .object({
    title: z.string().min(5, "Project title must be at least 5 characters."),
    description: z.string().min(20, "Description must be at least 20 characters."),
    requirements: z.string().min(1, "Requirements are required."),
    expectedDeliverables: z.string().min(1, "Expected deliverables are required."),
    pricingType: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(2),
    fixedPrice: z.coerce.number().optional(),
    minPrice: z.coerce.number().optional(),
    maxPrice: z.coerce.number().optional(),
    expectedDays: z.coerce.number().int().positive("Expected days must be greater than zero."),
    categoryId: z.coerce.number().int().positive().nullable().optional(),
    skillIds: z.array(z.coerce.number().int().positive()).default([]),
    customSkillNames: z.array(z.string().min(1)).default([]),
  })
  .superRefine((data, ctx) => {
    if (data.pricingType === 1) {
      if (!data.fixedPrice || Number(data.fixedPrice) <= 0) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
          path: ["fixedPrice"],
          message: "Fixed price is required and must be greater than 0 for fixed pricing.",
        });
      }
    }

    if (data.pricingType === 2) {
      if (!data.minPrice || Number(data.minPrice) <= 0) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
          path: ["minPrice"],
          message: "Min price is required and must be greater than 0 for range pricing.",
        });
      }

      if (!data.maxPrice || Number(data.maxPrice) <= Number(data.minPrice ?? 0)) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
          path: ["maxPrice"],
          message: "Max price is required and must be greater than min price for range pricing.",
        });
      }
    }
  });

---END FILE---

export default function ConfirmProjectModal({ project, onClose, onConfirm, pending }) {

"Saving..." : "Confirm"}</button></div></section></div>;
}

export default function DeleteProjectModal({ project, onClose, onConfirm, pending, isOpen = false }) {
  if (!isOpen || !project) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">
        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Archive project</h3>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-600">
          This will remove <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{project.title}</span> from your active project list. This action cannot be undone.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={onConfirm} disabled={pending} className="rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60">
            {pending ? "Archiving..." : "Archive"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useDeleteProject } from "../projectMutations.js";
import ProjectDeleteModal from "./ProjectDeleteModal.jsx";

export default function ProjectCard({ project }) {
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);
  const mutation = useDeleteProject();
  if (!project) return null;
  const budget = project.fixedPrice ?? project.minPrice ?? project.budgetMin ?? 0;
  return <><article className="project-card"><div><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">{project.status ?? "Open"}</span><h3>{project.title}</h3><p>{project.description}</p></div><div className="project-card__meta"><span>From ${Number(budget).toFixed(2)}</span><span>{project.expectedDays ?? project.deadlineDays ?? 0} days</span></div><div className="project-card__actions"><Link className="text-link" to={`/my-projects/${project.id}`}>View project</Link><button type="button" onClick={() => setDeleting(true)}>Archive</button></div></article><ProjectDeleteModal project={deleting ? project : null} pending={mutation.isPending} onClose={() => setDeleting(false)} onConfirm={() => mutation.mutate({ id: project.id }, { onSuccess: () => setDeleting(false) })} /></>;
}

import axiosInstance from "../../../../api/axiosInstance.js";

const normalizePage = (data) => Array.isArray(data) ? { results: data, count: data.length, page: 1, pageSize: data.length, totalPages: 1 } : data;

export const getSentInvitations = async (projectId) => normalizePage((await axiosInstance.get("/invitations/sent", { params: { projectId } })).data);
export const getProposals = async (projectId, params = {}) => normalizePage((await axiosInstance.get(`/proposals/project/${projectId}`, { params })).data);
export const rejectProposal = async (proposalId) => (await axiosInstance.patch(`/proposals/${proposalId}/reject`)).data;

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\client\projectManagement\__tests__\projectSchemas.test.js

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { projectCreateSchema } from "../projectSchemas.js";

describe("projectCreateSchema", () => {
  it("requires a fixed price when pricingType is fixed", () => {
    const result = projectCreateSchema.safeParse({
      title: "Landing page redesign",
      description: "This project requires a clean redesign for the marketing homepage.",
      requirements: "Modern homepage, responsive layout.",
      expectedDeliverables: "Updated homepage design and asset pack",
      pricingType: 1,
      expectedDays: 14,
      categoryId: 1,
      skillIds: [1],
      customSkillNames: [],
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error.issues.some((issue) => issue.path.includes("fixedPrice"))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("requires range values to be valid for hourly/range pricing", () => {
    const result = projectCreateSchema.safeParse({
      title: "Mobile app UI system",
      description: "We need a polished, responsive UI system for the mobile storefront.",
      requirements: "Components, specs, and onboarding flow.",
      expectedDeliverables: "Full UI kit and handoff docs",
      pricingType: 2,
      minPrice: 250,
      maxPrice: 250,
      expectedDays: 18,
      categoryId: 2,
      skillIds: [2],
      customSkillNames: [],
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error.issues.some((issue) => issue.path.includes("maxPrice"))).toBe(true);
  });
});

import { useState } from "react";
import { useContractDetail } from "./contractsQueries";
import { useSubmitWork, useRevisionAction, useDisputeMutation } from "./contractMutation";
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import formatMoney from "../../../utils/formatMoney";

export default function ContractDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { data: contract, isLoading, isError } = useContractDetail(id);
  const submit = useSubmitWork();
  const revision = useRevisionAction();
  const dispute = useDisputeMutation();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const [linkUrl, setLinkUrl] = useState("");
  if (isLoading) return <section className="content-section"><p>Loading contract…</p></section>;
  if (isError || !contract) return <section className="content-section"><p role="alert">Unable to load this contract.</p></section>;
  const value = (a, b) => contract[a] ?? contract[b];
  return <section className="content-section"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Contract #{id}</span><h1>{value("projectTitle", "project_title") || "Contract detail"}</h1><p>{value("clientName", "client_name")} · {value("status", "status")}</p><p>{formatMoney(value("totalAmount", "total_amount"))}</p><div className="card-list">{(value("deliverables", "deliverables") || []).map((item) => <article className="card" key={item.id}><strong>{item.title}</strong><p>{item.deliverableType || item.deliverable_type || "file"}</p><a href={item.linkUrl || item.link_url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Open deliverable</a></article>)}</div>{contract.status === "Active" && <form className="form-card" onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); submit.mutate({ id, payload: { title, notes, linkUrl, deliverableType: linkUrl ? "link" : "file" } }); }}><h2>Submit single deliverable</h2><input required value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Deliverable title" /><input value={linkUrl} onChange={(e) => setLinkUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://…" /><textarea value={notes} onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)} placeholder="Notes" /><button className="button button--accent" disabled={submit.isPending}>Submit work</button></form>}{(value("revisions", "revisions") || []).map((item) => <button key={item.id} className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => revision.mutate({ revisionId: item.id, action: "accept" })}>Accept revision</button>)}{["Active", "Submitted", "Disputed"].includes(contract.status) && <button className="button button--quiet" onClick={() => dispute.mutate({ contractId: id, reason: "Scope disagreement", description: "Raised from the contract workspace." })}>Raise dispute</button>}</section>;
}

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createDispute, postRevisionAction, submitContractWork } from "./contractsApi.js";
const useRefresh = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return () => { client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["contract"] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["freelancer-contracts"] }); }; };
export const useSubmitWork = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: submitContractWork, onSuccess: refresh }); };
export const useRevisionAction = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: postRevisionAction, onSuccess: refresh }); };
export const useDisputeMutation = () => { const refresh = useRefresh(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: createDispute, onSuccess: refresh }); };

import { useState } from "react";
import { useMyContracts } from "./contractsQueries";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import formatMoney from "../../../utils/formatMoney";

const statuses = ["All", "Offered", "Active", "Submitted", "Completed", "Disputed", "Cancelled"];
export default function Contracts() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("All");
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useMyContracts({ role: "freelancer", status: status === "All" ? undefined : status, page: 1, pageSize: 20 });
  const contracts = data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  return <section className="content-section"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Freelancer workspace</span><h1>My contracts</h1><p>Review signed work, delivery status, and payment readiness.</p><div className="button-row">{statuses.map((item) => <button key={item} className={`button ${status === item ? "button--accent" : "button--quiet"}`} onClick={() => setStatus(item)}>{item}</button>)}</div>{isLoading && <p>Loading contracts…</p>}{isError && <p role="alert">Unable to load contracts.</p>}{!isLoading && !isError && contracts.length === 0 && <p>No contracts found.</p>}<div className="card-list">{contracts.map((contract) => <article className="card" key={contract.id}><div><strong>{contract.projectTitle || contract.project_title || `Contract #${contract.id}`}</strong><p>{contract.freelancerName || contract.freelancer_name || "Freelancer"}</p></div><div><strong>{formatMoney(contract.totalAmount ?? contract.total_amount)}</strong><p>{contract.status}</p></div><button className="button button--secondary" onClick={() => navigate(`/freelancer/contracts/${contract.id}`)}>Open</button></article>)}</div></section>;
}

import axiosInstance from "../../../api/axiosInstance.js";
export const fetchAllContracts = async (params = {}) => (await axiosInstance.get("/contracts", { params })).data;
export const fetchContractDetail = async (id) => (await axiosInstance.get(`/contracts/${id}`)).data;
export const submitContractWork = async ({ id, payload }) => (await axiosInstance.post(`/deliverables/contract/${id}`, payload)).data;
export const postRevisionAction = async ({ revisionId, action, message }) => (await axiosInstance.post(`/revisions/${revisionId}/action`, { action, message })).data;
export const createDispute = async ({ contractId, reason, description, evidenceUrls = [] }) => (await axiosInstance.post(`/disputes/contract/${contractId}`, { reason, description, evidenceUrls })).data;

import { fetchAllContracts, fetchContractDetail } from "./contractsApi.js";
export const useMyContracts = (filters = {}) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["freelancer-contracts", filters], queryFn: () => fetchAllContracts(filters) });
export const useContractDetail = (id) => useQuery({ queryKey: ["contract", id], queryFn: () => fetchContractDetail(id), enabled: Boolean(id) });

---START FILE---
PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\freelancer\contracts\disputeForm.js
CONTENT:
import { z } from "zod";

export const disputeFormSchema = z.object({
  reason: z.string().trim().min(1, "A dispute reason is required."),
  description: z.string().trim().min(10, "Please provide at least 10 characters of detail."),
});

export function validateDisputeForm(values) {
  const result = disputeFormSchema.safeParse(values);
  return {
    valid: result.success,
    errors: result.success ? {} : Object.fromEntries(result.error.issues.map((issue) => [issue.path[0], issue.message])),
  };
}

export function toDisputePayload(contractId, values) {
  const result = validateDisputeForm(values);
  if (!result.valid) throw new Error("Invalid dispute form");
  return { contractId, reason: values.reason.trim(), description: values.description.trim(), evidenceUrls: [] };
}

---END FILE---

import { useState } from "react";
import { useDisputeMutation } from "../contractMutation";

export default function DisputeModal({ onClose, contractId }) {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const mutation = useDisputeMutation();
  return <div className="modal-backdrop" role="presentation"><form className="modal-card form-card" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); mutation.mutate({ contractId, reason, description }, { onSuccess: onClose }); }}><h2>Raise a dispute</h2><label>Reason<input required value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} /></label><label>Description<textarea required value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} /></label><div className="button-row"><button type="button" className="button button--quiet" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button><button className="button button--accent" disabled={mutation.isPending}>Submit dispute</button></div></form></div>;
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\freelancer\contracts\__tests__\disputeForm.test.js
CONTENT:
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { toDisputePayload, validateDisputeForm } from "../disputeForm.js";

describe("dispute creation form", () => {
  it("rejects missing or short values", () => {
    const result = validateDisputeForm({ reason: "", description: "short" });
    expect(result.valid).toBe(false);
    expect(result.errors.reason).toBeTruthy();
    expect(result.errors.description).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("accepts a valid reason and detailed description", () => {
    expect(validateDisputeForm({ reason: "Scope disagreement", description: "The requested work exceeds the agreed scope." }).valid).toBe(true);
  });

  it("builds the backend-compatible payload", () => {
    expect(toDisputePayload(42, { reason: " Scope disagreement ", description: " The requested work exceeds scope. " })).toEqual({
      contractId: 42,
      reason: "Scope disagreement",
      description: "The requested work exceeds scope.",
      evidenceUrls: [],
    });
  });

  it("does not build a payload for invalid input", () => {
    expect(() => toDisputePayload(42, { reason: "", description: "" })).toThrow("Invalid dispute form");
  });
});

---END FILE---

import axiosInstance from "../../../api/axiosInstance.js";

export const fetchRecommendedProjects = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/freelancers/recommended-projects")).data;
export const fetchTodos = async () => (await axiosInstance.get("/todos")).data;
export const createTodo = async (payload) => (await axiosInstance.post("/todos", JSON.stringify(typeof payload === "string" ? payload : payload.task), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } })).data;
export const deleteTodo = async (id) => (await axiosInstance.delete(`/todos/${id}`)).data;
export const completeTodo = async (id) => (await axiosInstance.patch(`/todos/${id}/toggle`)).data;

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { completeTodo, createTodo, deleteTodo } from "./DashboardApi.js";
export const useTodoMutations = () => {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const refresh = () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["todos"] });
  return { createTodo: useMutation({ mutationFn: createTodo, onSuccess: refresh }), deleteTodo: useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteTodo, onSuccess: refresh }), completeTodo: useMutation({ mutationFn: completeTodo, onSuccess: refresh }) };
};
export const useCreateTodo = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: createTodo, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["todos"] }) }); };
export const useDeleteTodo = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: deleteTodo, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["todos"] }) }); };
export const useCompleteTodo = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: completeTodo, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["todos"] }) }); };

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\freelancer\dashboard\components\ActiveContacts.jsx
CONTENT:
import { Calendar, DollarSign, User, FileX } from 'lucide-react';
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useMyContracts } from '../../contracts/contractsQueries';
import { formatDateDMY } from '../../../../utils/date';

function ActiveContacts() {
  const { data, isLoading } = useMyContracts();
  const contracts = data?.results || [];
  const activeContracts = contracts.filter((contract) => contract.status === 'active' || contract.status === 'disputed').slice(0, 4);

  const getDueDate = (signedAt, days) => {
    if (!signedAt || !days) return 'N/A';

    const signedDate = new Date(signedAt);
    const dueDate = new Date(signedDate);
    dueDate.setDate(signedDate.getDate() + parseInt(days, 10));

    return formatDateDMY(dueDate.toISOString());
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mb-8 w-full max-w-6xl">
        <h2 className="text-xl md:text-[25px] font-semibold text-[#111827] leading-7 mb-4 px-1">
          Active Contracts
        </h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:gap-6">
          {[...Array(2)].map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-40 bg-gray-100 animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-[#F3F4F6]" />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mb-8 w-full max-w-6xl">
      <h2 className="text-xl md:text-[25px] font-semibold text-[#111827] leading-7 mb-4 px-1">
        Active Contracts
      </h2>

      {activeContracts.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:gap-6">
          {activeContracts.map((contract, index) => (
            <div
              key={contract.id || index}
              className="bg-white rounded-xl p-5 md:p-6 border border-[#F3F4F6] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] md:text-xs text-[#6B7280] mb-2">
                <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 md:w-4 md:h-4 text-[#9CA3AF]" />
                <span className="truncate">{contract.client_name}</span>
              </div>

              <h3 className="text-sm md:text-[15px] font-bold text-[#111827] leading-tight md:leading-[22px] mb-3 line-clamp-2 min-h-[2.5rem] md:min-h-0">
                {contract.project_title}
              </h3>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] md:text-xs text-[#4B5563] mb-4">
                <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 md:w-4 md:h-4 text-primary" />
                <span>Due {getDueDate(contract.freelancer_signed_at, contract.delivery_days)}</span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-[#F3F4F6]">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <div className="p-1 bg-green-50 rounded">
                    <DollarSign className="w-3.5 h-3.5 md:w-4 md:h-4 text-green-600" />
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-[#111827]">
                    ${contract.total_amount}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <Link
                  to={`/freelancer/contracts/${contract.id}`}
                  className="text-[11px] md:text-xs font-bold text-primary hover:text-blue-700 transition-colors bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg"
                >
                  Details →
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-8 md:p-12 border border-[#F3F4F6] shadow-sm flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-gray-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
            <FileX className="w-7 h-7 text-gray-300" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-gray-600 font-bold text-sm md:text-base">No active contracts found.</p>
          <p className="text-[11px] md:text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Accept an offer to start a project.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default ActiveContacts;

import { ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useReceivedInvitations } from '../../invitations/invitationQueries';
import { formatDateDMY } from '../../../../utils/date';

function Invitations() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useReceivedInvitations();
  const invitations = data?.results || [];
  const displayInvitations = invitations.filter((inv) => inv.status === 'pending').slice(0, 5);

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 mb-8 shadow-sm border border-[#F3F4F6] text-center">
        <div className="animate-pulse space-y-4">
          <div className="h-6 bg-gray-100 rounded w-1/4 mx-auto" />
          <div className="h-20 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg" />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isError) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 mb-8 shadow-sm border border-[#F3F4F6] text-center">
        <p className="text-red-500 font-medium">Failed to load invitations.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-4 md:p-6 mb-8 shadow-sm border border-[#F3F4F6]">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl md:text-[25px] font-bold text-[#1E293B] tracking-tight">

          View All
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 md:gap-4">
        {displayInvitations.length > 0 ? (
          displayInvitations.map((invitation, index) => (
            <div
              key={index}
              className="group flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#E2E8F0] hover:border-[#4F46E5]/30 hover:bg-slate-50/50 transition-all cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start md:items-center gap-3 md:gap-4 mb-3 sm:mb-0">
                <div className={`shrink-0 w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center ${index === 1 ? 'bg-[#DCFCE7]' : 'bg-[#E0E7FF]'}`}>
                  {index === 1 ? (
                    <svg className="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6" viewBox="0 0 25 28" fill="none">
                      <path d="M8.12111 19.8335L2.28778 14.0002L8.12111 8.16683L9.50653 9.55225L5.03431 14.0245L9.48223 18.4724L8.12111 19.8335ZM15.8989 19.8335L14.5135 18.4481L18.9857 13.9759L14.5378 9.52794L15.8989 8.16683L21.7322 14.0002L15.8989 19.8335Z" fill="#16A34A" />
                    </svg>

                    <svg className="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6" viewBox="0 0 25 28" fill="none">
                      <path d="M8.89889 12.9791L10.9892 10.8645L9.62806 9.47911L8.55861 10.5486L7.1975 9.18745L8.24264 8.118L7.14889 7.02425L5.03431 9.13883L8.89889 12.9791ZM16.8711 20.9756L18.9857 18.8611L17.8919 17.7673L16.8225 18.8124L15.4614 17.4513L16.5065 16.3819L15.1211 15.0208L13.0308 17.1111L16.8711 20.9756ZM7.39195 22.7499H3.26V18.618L7.51348 14.3645L2.28778 9.13883L7.14889 4.27772L12.3989 9.52772L16.069 5.83328C16.2635 5.63883 16.4822 5.493 16.7253 5.39578C16.9683 5.29856 17.2195 5.24994 17.4788 5.24994C17.738 5.24994 17.9892 5.29856 18.2322 5.39578C18.4753 5.493 18.694 5.63883 18.8885 5.83328L20.1767 7.14578C20.3711 7.34022 20.5169 7.55897 20.6142 7.80203C20.7114 8.04508 20.76 8.29624 20.76 8.5555C20.76 8.81476 20.7114 9.06187 20.6142 9.29682C20.5169 9.53177 20.3711 9.74647 20.1767 9.94092L16.5065 13.6354L21.7322 18.8611L16.8711 23.7222L11.6454 18.4965L7.39195 22.7499ZM5.20445 20.8055H6.56556L16.0933 11.302L14.7079 9.91661L5.20445 19.4444V20.8055ZM15.4128 10.6215L14.7079 9.91661L16.0933 11.302L15.4128 10.6215Z" fill="#4F46E5" />
                    </svg>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <h3 className="text-sm md:text-base font-bold text-[#1E293B] leading-tight truncate">
                    {invitation.project_title}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs md:text-sm font-medium text-[#64748B] mt-0.5">
                    {invitation.client_name} <span className="mx-1 text-gray-300">•</span> {formatDateDMY(invitation.created_at)}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between sm:justify-end gap-3 sm:pl-4">
                <span className="bg-[#FEF9C3] text-[#CA8A04] px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                  {invitation.status}
                </span>
                <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-[#64748B] group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center py-10 border-2 border-dashed border-[#E2E8F0] rounded-2xl bg-gray-50/50">
            <p className="text-[#64748B] font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-widest">No pending invitations</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Briefcase } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useRecommendedProjects } from '../dashboardQueries';

function JobRecommendation() {
  const { data, isLoading } = useRecommendedProjects();
  const jobList = data?.results || [];

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mb-8 w-full">
        <h2 className="text-xl md:text-[25px] font-semibold text-[#111827] leading-7 mb-4 px-1">
          Recommended Jobs
        </h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-6">
          {[...Array(3)].map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-64 bg-gray-100 animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-[#F3F4F6]" />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mb-8 w-full">
      <h2 className="text-xl md:text-[25px] font-semibold text-[#111827] leading-7 mb-4 px-1">
        Recommended Jobs
      </h2>

      {jobList.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-6">
          {jobList.map((job, index) => (
            <div key={job.id || index} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#F3F4F6] shadow-sm flex flex-col h-full hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
              <div className="p-5 md:p-6 lg:p-8 flex flex-col h-full">
                <div className="mb-4">
                  <span className="inline-block bg-blue-50 text-primary px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] md:text-[12px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                    {job.category}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <h3 className="text-base md:text-[18px] lg:text-[20px] font-bold text-[#111827] leading-tight mb-3 line-clamp-2 min-h-[2.5rem] md:min-h-0">
                  {job.title}
                </h3>

                <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-[#4B5563] leading-relaxed mb-6 line-clamp-3 flex-grow">
                  {job.description}
                </p>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-5 border-b border-[#F3F4F6]">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] md:text-[13px] text-gray-400 mb-0.5 font-bold uppercase tracking-tighter">Budget</p>
                    <p className="text-sm md:text-base font-black text-gray-900">${job.budget}</p>

                  </div>

                  {job.skills?.slice(0, 3).map((skill, i) => (
                    <span key={i} className="bg-[#F9FAFB] text-[#4B5563] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-gray-100 text-[10px] md:text-[11px] font-bold">
                      {skill}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                  {job.skills?.length > 3 && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 self-center">+{job.skills.length - 3}</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <Link
                  to={`/freelancer/jobs/${job.id}`}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-full md:w-auto md:justify-start text-xs font-black text-primary hover:text-blue-700 transition-colors mt-auto group"
                >
                  View Details
                  <span className="ml-1 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform">→</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 md:p-12 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-gray-50 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-4 rotate-3">

          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1 max-w-[200px] md:max-w-none">
            Update your profile skills to get better recommendations.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { Home, Briefcase, FileText, MessageSquare, CheckSquare, Tag, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';

const menuItems = [
  { icon: Home, label: 'Dashboard', path: '/freelancer' },
  { icon: Briefcase, label: 'Find Jobs', path: '/freelancer/jobs' },
  { icon: FileText, label: 'Contracts', path: '/freelancer/contracts' },
  { icon: MessageSquare, label: 'Proposals', path: '/freelancer/my-proposals' },
  { icon: CheckSquare, label: 'Invitations', path: '/freelancer/invitations' },
  { icon: Tag, label: 'Offers', path: '/freelancer/offers' },
];

export function Sidebar({ isOpen, onClose }) {
  const location = useLocation();

  return (
    <aside className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 w-64 bg-white border-r border-[#E5E7EB] flex flex-col shrink-0 transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out lg:relative lg:translate-x-0 ${isOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'}`}>
      <div className="h-[71px] md:h-[81px] border-b border-[#E5E7EB] flex items-center justify-between px-6">
        <h2 className="text-[18px] md:text-[20.4px] font-bold text-[#111827]">Freelancer Panel</h2>
        <button onClick={onClose} className="lg:hidden p-1 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded">
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <nav className="flex-1 px-4 py-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {menuItems.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            const isActive = location.pathname === item.path;

            return (
              <Link
                key={item.path}
                to={item.path}
                onClick={onClose}
                className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg transition-colors ${isActive ? 'bg-[#3B82F6] text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-200' : 'text-[#374151] hover:bg-gray-50'}`}
              >
                <Icon className="w-5 h-5" strokeWidth={2} />
                <span className="text-sm font-medium">{item.label}</span>
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </nav>
    </aside>
  );
}

export default Sidebar;

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\freelancer\dashboard\components\TodoList.jsx
CONTENT:
import React from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Trash2, Plus, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTodos } from '../dashboardQueries';
import { useTodoMutations } from '../dashboardMutations';

function TodoList() {
  const { data: tasksData, isLoading } = useTodos();
  const { createTodo, deleteTodo, completeTodo, isCreating } = useTodoMutations();

  const tasks = tasksData?.results || [];
  const totalCount = tasksData?.count || 0;
  const isLimitReached = totalCount >= 10;

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm({
    defaultValues: {
      todo: '',
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    if (!isCreating && !isLimitReached) {
      createTodo({ todo: data.todo }, {
        onSuccess: () => reset(),
      });
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#F3F4F6] p-6 shadow-sm h-full flex items-center justify-center">
        <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#F3F4F6] p-6 shadow-sm h-full flex flex-col">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-[22px] font-bold text-[#111827] leading-7" style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter, sans-serif' }}>
          Personal Tasks
        </h2>
        <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded ${isLimitReached ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600' : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-600'}`}>
          {totalCount}/10 Slots
        </span>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="mb-6">
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <div className="flex-1 relative">
            <input
              type="text"
              {...register('todo', {
                required: 'Task is required',
                maxLength: { value: 60, message: 'Limit 60 characters' },
              })}
              placeholder={isLimitReached ? 'Limit reached (10/10)' : 'Add a new task...'}
              disabled={isLimitReached || isCreating}
              className={`w-full h-[42px] px-4 border ${errors.todo ? 'border-red-500' : 'border-[#E5E7EB]'} rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#3B82F6] transition-all disabled:bg-gray-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed`}
            />
          </div>
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={isLimitReached || isCreating}
            className="bg-[#3B82F6] text-white px-4 h-[42px] rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-[#2563EB] transition-colors shadow-sm disabled:bg-gray-300 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            {isCreating ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />}
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold hidden sm:inline">Add</span>
          </button>
        </div>
        {errors.todo && <p className="text-red-500 text-[10px] mt-1 ml-1">{errors.todo.message}</p>}
      </form>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto max-h-[400px] pr-2 custom-scrollbar flex-1">
        {tasks.length > 0 ? (
          tasks.map((task) => (
            <div
              key={task.id}
              className="group flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-transparent md:hover:bg-[#F9FAFB] border border-transparent md:hover:border-[#F3F4F6] transition-all"
            >
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={task.is_completed}
                onChange={() => completeTodo(task.id)}
                className="w-5 h-5 rounded accent-[#3B82F6] cursor-pointer flex-shrink-0"
              />
              <span className={`flex-1 text-sm transition-all break-words min-w-0 ${task.is_completed ? 'text-[#9CA3AF] line-through' : 'text-[#374151] font-medium'}`}>
                {task.todo}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => deleteTodo(task.id)}
                className="p-2 md:p-1.5 opacity-100 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-500 rounded-md transition-all text-[#9CA3AF] flex-shrink-0"
                title="Delete task"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 md:w-4 md:h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center py-10">
            <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm italic">No tasks for today!</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {isLimitReached && (
        <p className="mt-4 text-[11px] text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 p-2 rounded border border-amber-100">
          Task limit reached. Delete a task to add a new one.
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default TodoList;

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAllJobs } from "./findJobsQueries.js";

export default function FindJobs() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useAllJobs();
  const jobs = data?.results || data || [];

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading opportunities…</div>;
  if (isError) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-600">Failed to load opportunities.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-blue-600">Freelancer</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Find work</h1>
      </div>

      {jobs.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-12 text-center text-slate-500">No opportunities available right now.</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
          {jobs.map((job) => (
            <article key={job.id ?? job.Id} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900">{job.title ?? job.Title}</h2>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">{job.categoryName ?? job.category ?? "General"}</p>
                </div>
                <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700">${Number(job.fixedPrice ?? job.minPrice ?? job.maxPrice ?? 0).toLocaleString()}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-slate-600 line-clamp-3">{job.description ?? job.summary ?? "No summary provided."}</p>
              <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">{job.status ?? "Open"}</span>
                <Link to={`/jobs/${job.id ?? job.Id}`} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700">View details</Link>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export const fetchFreelancerProjects = async (params = {}) => normalizePage((await axiosInstance.get("/projects", { params })).data);

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fetchFreelancerProjects } from "./findJobsAPI.js";

export const useFreelancerProjects = (filters = {}) =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: ["freelancer-projects", filters],
    queryFn: () => fetchFreelancerProjects(filters),
  });

export const useAllJobs = (filters = {}) => useFreelancerProjects(filters);

import axiosInstance from "../../../api/axiosInstance.js";
const normalizePage = (data) => Array.isArray(data) ? { results: data, count: data.length, page: 1, pageSize: data.length, totalPages: 1 } : data;
export const getReceivedInvitations = async (params = {}) => normalizePage((await axiosInstance.get("/invitations/received", { params })).data);
export const updateInvitationStatus = async ({ id, status }) => (await axiosInstance.patch(`/invitations/${id}/status`, { status })).data;

export const useUpdateInvitationStatus = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: updateInvitationStatus, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["received-invitations"] }) }); };

import { useReceivedInvitations } from "./invitationQueries.js";
import { useUpdateInvitationStatus } from "./invitationMutations.js";

export default function InvitationsPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useReceivedInvitations();
  const mutation = useUpdateInvitationStatus();
  const invitations = data?.results || data || [];

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading invitations…</div>;
  if (isError) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-600">Failed to load invitations.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-blue-600">Freelancer</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Invitations</h1>
      </div>

      {invitations.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-12 text-center text-slate-500">No invitations yet.</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {invitations.map((invite) => (
            <article key={invite.id ?? invite.Id} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{invite.projectTitle ?? invite.project?.title ?? invite.projectName ?? "Project invite"}</h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{invite.message ?? "You have been invited to this project."}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: invite.id ?? invite.Id, status: "accepted" })} className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-emerald-700">Accept</button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: invite.id ?? invite.Id, status: "declined" })} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Decline</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useJobDetails } from "./jobDetailsQueries.js";
import ProposalModal from "../proposals/components/ProposalModal.jsx";
import { useState } from "react";

export default function JobDetails() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useJobDetails(id);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading opportunity…</div>;
  if (isError || !data) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-600">Opportunity not found.</div>;

  const budget = Number(data.fixedPrice ?? data.minPrice ?? data.maxPrice ?? 0);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-blue-600">Opportunity</p>
            <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{data.title ?? data.Title}</h1>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700">Send proposal</button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[2fr,1fr]">
          <div className="space-y-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-500">Description</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 text-slate-700">{data.description ?? data.summary ?? "No details provided."}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4"><p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Budget</p><p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">${budget.toLocaleString()}</p></div>
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4"><p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Delivery</p><p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{data.expectedDays ?? 0} days</p></div>
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4"><p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Category</p><p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{data.categoryName ?? data.category ?? "General"}</p></div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Requirements</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-line text-slate-700">{data.requirements || "No requirements specified."}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <aside className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-500">Project summary</h2>
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-sm text-slate-600">
              <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span>Budget</span><strong className="text-slate-900">${budget.toLocaleString()}</strong></div>
              <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span>Timeline</span><strong className="text-slate-900">{data.expectedDays ?? 0} days</strong></div>
              <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span>Status</span><strong className="text-slate-900">{data.status ?? "Open"}</strong></div>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>

      <ProposalModal projectId={Number(id)} isOpen={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />
    </>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useFreelancerOffers } from "./offersQueries.js";
import { useModal } from "../../../hooks/modal/useModalStore.jsx";

export default function FreelancerOffers() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useFreelancerOffers();
  const { openModal } = useModal();
  const offers = data?.results || (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
  if (isLoading) return <section className="content-section"><p>Loading offers…</p></section>;
  if (isError) return <section className="content-section"><p role="alert">Unable to load offers.</p></section>;
  return <section className="content-section"><span className="app-shell__eyebrow">Freelancer workspace</span><h1>Project offers</h1><p>Review and sign contracts to begin work.</p><div className="card-list">{offers.map((offer) => <article className="card" key={offer.id}><div><strong>{offer.projectTitle || offer.project_title || `Offer #${offer.id}`}</strong><p>{offer.clientName || offer.client_name || "Client"}</p></div><p>{offer.status}</p><button className="button button--accent" onClick={() => openModal("offer-modal", { offer })}>Review & sign</button></article>)}</div>{offers.length === 0 && <p>No offers are waiting for you.</p>}</section>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useAcceptOffer } from "./offersMutations";

export default function OfferModal({ onClose, offer }) {
  const [signature, setSignature] = useState("");
  const [agreed, setAgreed] = useState(false);
  const mutation = useAcceptOffer();
  return <div className="modal-backdrop" role="presentation"><form className="modal-card form-card" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); mutation.mutate({ contractId: offer.id, data: { signature, signatureType: "text" } }, { onSuccess: onClose }); }}><h2>Review and sign offer</h2><p>{offer.projectTitle || offer.project_title} · {offer.totalAmount || offer.total_amount} USD</p><label>Digital signature<input required value={signature} onChange={(e) => setSignature(e.target.value)} /></label><label><input type="checkbox" checked={agreed} onChange={(e) => setAgreed(e.target.checked)} /> I accept the contract terms</label><div className="button-row"><button type="button" className="button button--quiet" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button><button className="button button--accent" disabled={!agreed || mutation.isPending}>Sign and start</button></div></form></div>;
}

import { acceptContract } from "./offersApi.js";
export const useAcceptOffer = () => { const client = useQueryClient(); return useMutation({ mutationFn: acceptContract, onSuccess: () => { client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["freelancer-offers"] }); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["freelancer-contracts"] }); } }); };

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\freelancer\profile\EarningsOverview.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useFreelancerTransactions } from "./profileQueries.js";

export default function EarningsOverview() {
  const { data: transactions = [], isLoading } = useFreelancerTransactions();

  const rows = Array.isArray(transactions?.results) ? transactions.results : Array.isArray(transactions) ? transactions : [];

  const totalEarned = rows.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.amount ?? item.total ?? 0), 0);
  const pending = rows.filter((item) => String(item.status ?? "").toLowerCase() === "pending").reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.amount ?? 0), 0);
  const paid = rows.filter((item) => ["paid", "completed", "approved"].includes(String(item.status ?? "").toLowerCase())).reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.amount ?? 0), 0);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-sm text-slate-500">Loading earnings...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-6 px-4 py-10">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Earnings overview</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Track the flow of paid and pending client settlements.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-slate-500">Total earned</p>
          <p className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">${totalEarned.toFixed(2)}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-slate-500">Pending</p>
          <p className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">${pending.toFixed(2)}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-slate-500">Paid</p>
          <p className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">${paid.toFixed(2)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Transaction history</h2>
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-200 text-left text-sm text-slate-700">
            <thead className="bg-slate-50">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Date</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Status</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Amount</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200 bg-white">
              {rows.length > 0 ? (
                rows.map((row, index) => (
                  <tr key={`${row.id || row.reference || index}`}>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">{row.date || row.created_at || "—"}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 capitalize">{row.status || "pending"}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">${Number(row.amount ?? row.total ?? 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              ) : (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan="3" className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-slate-400">No transactions yet.</td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useUpdatePaymentSettings } from './profileMutation';

export default function EditPaymentModal({ isOpen, onClose, currentEmail }) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(currentEmail || '');
  const { mutate, isLoading } = useUpdatePaymentSettings();

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    mutate(
      { paypal_email: email },
      { onSuccess: () => onClose() },
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl max-w-md w-full overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center px-6 py-4 border-b">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">Update PayPal Email</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-full transition">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6">
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase mb-1">PayPal Email Address</label>
              <input
                type="email"
                required
                autoFocus
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                placeholder="yourname@example.com"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-8 flex gap-3">
            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 font-medium">
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isLoading}
              className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {isLoading && <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />}
              Update
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

PATH: C:\catacolite\webofwork\client\src\features\freelancer\profile\PortfolioModal.jsx
CONTENT:
import { useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { Loader2, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { useCreatePortfolioItem } from "../../client/onboarding/onBoardingMutations.js";

export default function PortfolioModal({ isOpen, onClose, onAdded }) {
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const { register, handleSubmit, reset } = useForm({
    defaultValues: {
      title: "",
      description: "",
      imageUrl: "",
    },
  });

  const { mutateAsync: createPortfolioItem } = useCreatePortfolioItem();

  const onSubmit = async (data) => {
    try {
      setIsSubmitting(true);
      const created = await createPortfolioItem({
        title: data.title,
        description: data.description,
        imageUrl: data.imageUrl,
      });
      onAdded?.(created);
      reset();
      onClose();
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/40 p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Add portfolio item</h3>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="text-sm text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700">Close</button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Title</label>
            <input {...register("title", { required: true })} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Description</label>
            <textarea {...register("description", { required: true })} rows={4} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Image URL</label>
            <input {...register("imageUrl", { required: true })} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100" />
          </div>

          <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300">
            {isSubmitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />} Save item
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
